- **Interest (관심) = the shell computes it from its own layout (amendment 6 — user decision 2026-09-27 · §3 #78):** the layout is already the shell's — windows and tabs and the active tab (`WindowTabs{tabs, active}` `layout/manager.rs:99-108` · window table `:135`) · the slot tree · slot content (ADR-0035). The companies the usage slot turned on also live in slot content — `SlotContent::Usage{show_claude, show_codex}` (「슬롯」 above · toggle = ~~whole replacement of `set_slot_content` `src/store/viewStore.ts:213-219`~~ a `set_usage_slot` write that sends only its own cell as an absolute value (amendment 17 — main decision 2026-09-30 · §3 #102 ⑤) · ~~the menu ☑ reads the ctx's `node.content` — §1-8 「메뉴」~~ the on/off row ☑ of the popup reads slot content — §1-8 「팝업」, amendment 15). ★There is no toggle state that lives only in the webview★. So the webview neither computes nor reports interest — the report was a second copy of what the shell has, and the order of that copy had to be kept as a rule (amendments 3–5 · §3 #78).
  - **Two pure functions (`usage_interest.rs` — knows nothing of Tauri, sockets or clocks · `lib_unit`):** `interest_from_layout(mgr: &ViewManager, hidden: &BTreeSet<WindowLabel>) -> BTreeSet<AgentBackendKind>` = among the windows the shell knows (`mgr.windows`), for each window not in `hidden`, the union of the companies turned on by the usage slot in the tree of **that window's active tab** (`active`) · `usage_slot_windows(mgr) -> BTreeSet<WindowLabel>` = a window that has a usage slot in any tab (including non-active tabs and hidden windows) — the windows that will receive broadcasts (「방송 전달」 below). ★Visible = the active tab of a window with no hidden marker★ — minimizing is not tracked by the shell, so it counts as visible (user decision 2026-09-27 · §2). A view not bound to any window (the temporary view of slot-move phase A — rollback `apply.rs:745-754`) is not a tab of any window and so naturally drops out. ★A window that remains only in the model (a known unresolved item of `apply.rs:101-114` — a case where a window and tabs remain in the model without an OS window) counts as visible★ — it is resolved together when that defect is resolved.
  - **Recompute places (재계산 자리) = everywhere the layout or visibility changes, inside the ViewManager lock:** ① the real `RouterSubs::resync` of `SubscriptionSync` (`commands/layout.rs:74-78` — the `OwnedSubs` of the LLM table also hands it to this `:136-143`) calls `client.usage_layout_changed(mgr)` after the output routing recompute. Of the writes of the apply service, every one that receives this port comes here — tab create, close and switch · window create (including rollback) and close · slot split and close · agent assignment · slot content set (`set_slot_content` `apply.rs:449-468` · the new `set_usage_slot` also receives the same port) · spawn · moving a slot to another window (each step of `move_slot_to_window` · temporary-view rollback). Writes that do not receive the port (`focus_slot`·`set_split_ratio`·`rename_tab`·two measurement reports) do not change slot content, tabs or windows, so interest does not change either (`apply.rs:22-31`). ② popup window destruction `cleanup_popup_window` (`commands/popout.rs:194-205` — right after `cleanup_window_core`, inside the same lock). ③ tray hide and show (「숨김」 below). ★Lock order = `ViewManager → 사용량 관심 → lifecycle` (amendment 7)★ — the usage interest lock is **not** a leaf: what it does inside is only in-memory computation · nudge `try_enqueue` · starting a shrink-timer task, but the nudge `try_enqueue` takes the lifecycle lock with `current_cmd_tx()` (`daemon_client/mod.rs:871-882` · `daemon_client/lifecycle.rs:335-341`). ★lifecycle never calls back either way★ — there is no place that takes the usage interest or ViewManager lock inside the lifecycle lock, so the order is one-directional (acyclic). ViewManager{lifecycle} nesting already exists — the unsubscribe path (`src-tauri/src/commands/popout.rs:195-201`). The connection task takes only the usage interest lock and does not take the ViewManager lock, so there is no reverse order. The port contract (「락 안 · 동기 · 비블로킹」 `apply.rs:71-80`) is kept as is, and 「레이아웃에서 파생되는 사용량 관심 재계산도 같은 자리」 is added to its doc. ★The tray path also takes the ViewManager lock briefly★ — the precedent for taking that lock on the event loop thread is `cleanup_popup_window` (only the window host port must be outside the lock — `apply.rs:90-99`).
  - ★LLM control — no new handle (CLAUDE.md 「LLM-우선 제어」)★: turning the usage slot on and off · tab switch · slot move · window close, done with the existing layout commands (`layout.setSlotContent`·`tab.*`·`window.*`·`slot.*` — the same apply service as a human click, ADR-0081 decision 3), make the recompute above drive the subscription by itself. No subscription-only command is set — ⟳ already has the bus `usage.refresh` (§1-6).
- **Shell state machine (셸 상태기) — new `src-tauri/src/daemon_client/usage_interest.rs` (amendment 6 · pure — knows nothing of Tauri, sockets or clocks, the caller supplies the time):** `hidden: 셸이 숨긴 창 label 들` (「숨김」 below · the label of a destroyed window is erased by the recompute of `cleanup_popup_window` — 「창 소멸」 below) · `interest: 지금 관심` (`interest_from_layout` of the last recompute) · `usage_windows: 사용량 슬롯이 있는 창` (`usage_slot_windows` of the last recompute) · `socket_epoch: 지금 소켓의 표식` (0 = no socket — 「소켓 표식」 below) · `max_seen_socket_epoch: 본 가장 큰 소켓 표식` (`on_socket_open` below) · `sent: 이 소켓에 마지막으로 보낸 집합` · `cache: 회사 → {최고 revision 스냅숏, 받은 시각}` (of this socket) · shrink generation numberer + `shrink_pending: Option<세대>` (a pending shrink — stands only until the 1.5-second deadline). The shell opens one daemon connection, so the connection cell of the cache key (connection, company) is 「지금 소켓」 — that is, `socket_epoch`. Methods (all return only a result, and the socket write, emit and timer start are the caller's share — the nudge `try_enqueue` is non-blocking so it may be inside the lock · but it takes the lifecycle lock inside it, so this lock is not a leaf — order = `ViewManager → 사용량 관심 → lifecycle`, 「재계산 자리」 above):
  - `recompute(mgr, now) -> action` · `set_visible(label, bool, mgr, now) -> action` (the same recompute after fixing the hidden marker) — recompute `interest`·`usage_windows` and ★`action` comes from comparing against `sent`★: if the interest has a company not in `sent`, nudge (at once · `shrink_pending := None`) · if interest ⊊ `sent`, `Defer(세대)` (`shrink_pending := Some(세대)` — after `USAGE_INTEREST_SHRINK_DELAY = 1.5초`) · if equal, none (`shrink_pending := None` — a pending shrink is invalidated). ★Amendment 14 — 6a review follow-up (§3 #91): if a pending shrink already stands and the interest is the same as the previous recompute, a new generation is not drawn and the existing generation and deadline are left as they are★ — so that an unrelated recompute (one that does not change interest) cannot push back a shrink timer that is already running. A new generation is drawn only when the interest changes (and is still a proper subset of `sent`). While a tab is detached to another window (dropping out of the old window → entering the new one), the interest returns to `sent`, so nothing goes to the daemon. ★`action` does not carry the set★ — the set to send is read at send time (「한 직렬 경로」 below).
  - `deferral_elapsed(세대) -> bool` — called by the shrink timer (1.5 seconds): if `shrink_pending == Some(세대)`, lower it to `None` and `true` (→ the timer sends one nudge). ★A pending shrink ends **at the deadline**, not on delivery★ — even if that nudge disappears (command channel saturated — `try_enqueue` `daemon_client/mod.rs:879-881`), the wait is already over, so the reconciliation of the next snapshot fixes it by comparing against the interest (「대조」 below).
  - `sync(socket_epoch, force) -> Option<집합>` — ★only the connection task calls it★ (`force = false` when taking out a nudge · `force = true` just before writing the `RefreshUsageLimits` frame — 「⟳」 below, amendment 7). If it is not the current marker, `None`. If `force`, always `Some(interest)` · otherwise, when there is a pending shrink, only when the interest has a company outside `sent` (pure shrinking is the pending wait's share), and when there is none, `Some(interest)` if interest ≠ `sent`. If `Some`, then `sent := interest` · `shrink_pending := None`. It is based on `sent` so it gives the same result when called repeatedly (idempotent — the nudge right after the save of 「새 소켓」 below relies on it).
  - `on_socket_open(socket_epoch) -> Option<집합>` — ★only when `socket_epoch > max_seen_socket_epoch`★ — otherwise it does nothing (`None`): so that a late open/close of a task pushed out by succession cannot overwrite the new socket's cache and `sent`. If it passes, `max_seen_socket_epoch`·`socket_epoch` replaced · cache cleared · `sent := interest` · `shrink_pending := None` · if not empty, `Some` (to be sent directly to the new socket — below). `on_socket_lost(socket_epoch)` — only when `socket_epoch` now: cache cleared · `sent := ∅` · `socket_epoch := 0` (`max_seen_socket_epoch` is left as is).
  - `on_snapshot(socket_epoch, snapshot, subscribed, now) -> {labels, resend}` — discarded if it is not `socket_epoch` now (`labels` empty · no `resend`) · ★cache = the **highest** revision per company★: even if a smaller revision arrives late, it does not replace — because the daemon sends the first snapshot outside the registry lock (§1-4 「첫 스냅숏 순서」), the last arrival may not be the highest. If equal or greater, replace, and the received time = `now` · `labels` = `usage_windows` (hidden windows · including a window that has the usage slot only in a non-active tab — when shown again it is the latest at once) · delivery is done regardless of revision (the frontend tells them apart — §1-8) · `resend` = 「대조」 below.
  - `snapshot_for_webview(now) -> {socket_epoch, snapshots}` — called by the webview pull (「웹뷰가 값을 받는 길」 below): hands over the whole cache through 「핸드오프 보정」 below (no daemon round trip).
  - ★Hand-off correction (핸드오프 보정)★: when handing over a copy of the cache, the relative cells are shifted by the whole seconds `d` elapsed since receipt — per window (including per-model) `age_secs + d` · `Failed.next_attempt_in_secs`·`Rejected.retry_in_secs` is `− d` (saturating at 0). If this is not done, an old cache (up to 15 minutes) would show the value that much younger and the wait that much longer (the frontend accepts a late-arriving copy of the same revision — §1-8).
- **Reconciliation (대조) — match with the `subscribed` the daemon carried (amendment 6 — user decision 2026-09-27 「클로드만 와도 상태플래그 하나 더 와서 안맞으면 싱크」):** every `UsageLimitsUpdated` the daemon sends to a connection carries the **current** company set `subscribed` of that connection (§1-4 「구독 명부」·「발행」 · §1-6 — including the first snapshot of a newly added company). `on_snapshot` compares it with the set the shell wants the daemon to have — `sent` if there is a pending shrink (`shrink_pending`), `interest` if not. ★When they differ, the set sent back is that wanted set (amendment 7)★: if there is a pending shrink, it is `resend = Some(sent)` and ★the pending wait is left as is★ (`shrink_pending`·`sent` unchanged — one stale snapshot that arrives inside the 1.5-second window must not fire the shrink early · the shrink is the deadline's share) · only when there is no wait, `resend = Some(interest)` · `sent := interest` → the call site writes one `UsageSubscribe{그 집합}` to that socket (「방송 전달」 below). ★It sends back every time they diverge — no suppression (amendment 8)★: **each and every** snapshot whose `subscribed` differs from the wanted set yields one resend, and each time leaves one `debug` log line (`subscribed` · the wanted set). Three reasons it is safe — ① a same-set replacement issues no first snapshot, and a latch emit is only once per window, so the feedback is finite — in the daemon a replacement to the same set has no newly added company and so issues no first snapshot (§1-4 「구독 명부」). ★One exception — the latch★: since the subscription path evaluates the expiry latch of the requested companies **before** the registry lock (§1-4 event table ①), if a window has a passed reset time one emit goes out, and its `subscribed` is the **pre**-replacement set, so it yields one more resend of the same set. That latch stands only once per window, so the next replacement issues nothing ② the cost is bounded by the frequency of incoming events (at most one small frame per event) ③ even a difference that is never filled (e.g. a daemon normalization defect) is capped at one frame per event and shows in the `debug` log. ★The 「같은 짝 억제 가드」 of amendment 7 is removed (amendment 7 re-review — both reviewers)★: both an old record left by an earlier toggle round trip, and a correction resend the daemon refused (receiving line saturated), carried the same pair again and blocked a needed resend, and the divergence did not heal (§3 #84). ★This one rule replaces the old two `on_snapshot` resend rules (a company outside `sent` → `sent` resend · healing of an expired shrink) (§3 #78)★ — it catches both 「켰는데 안 온다」 (a company missing in the daemon — triggered by another company's snapshot) and 「껐는데 온다」 (a company left in the daemon → unsubscribe — triggered by that company's snapshot). The broadcast of a company that will drop out during the shrink delay is legitimate because the daemon still subscribes to the old `sent`, so it is not touched with `subscribed` = `sent`. ★The crossing is only one extra sheet★ — a snapshot that left before a replacement traveling on the socket (or before the one shrink sheet right after the 1.5-second deadline) carries the old set and makes the same interest be sent one more time, but a same-set replacement issues no first snapshot in the daemon since no company is newly added, so the feedback is finite (the latch exception is ① above). Both sets are canonical words — the shell's interest comes only from the two cells `show_claude`·`show_codex` and so is inside the daemon registry (§1-6 key normalization). ★Limitation — if a connection has **no** subscription at all, no snapshot arrives and there is no occasion to reconcile (§6 #25)★ — ⟳ fixes it (below).
- **⟳ = manual sync (amendment 6 — user decision 2026-09-27 「체크온오프하고 동기화버튼 정도로 매칭」):** the webview ⟳ sends `RefreshUsageLimits` through the shell's generic path `forward_daemon_command` (`commands/agent.rs:207`) — the shell passes it as usual to the waiting `send_command` (`:264`). ★Subscription and refresh are one action of the connection task (amendment 7)★: when the connection task takes `RefreshUsageLimits` out of the command channel and writes that frame, it **first** calls `sync(socket_epoch, force=true)` and writes `UsageSubscribe{interest}` (idempotent in the daemon if the set is the same) to the same socket, and then writes the refresh frame. ★Write order = subscription frame → registration of the refresh's PendingMap → refresh frame (amendment 8)★ — the generic `SendCommand` arm registers pending **first** and then writes (`src-tauri/src/daemon_client/connection.rs:1083` registration → `:1086` write · write failure = take it back out and wake with `SEND_FAILED_PREFIX` `:1088-1092`). The subscription frame is written before that registration: if the subscription write fails, the refresh comes back as an **unsent failure** with neither registration nor write — ★before leaving the loop, the connection task wakes that refresh's `reply` oneshot with the same `SEND_FAILED_PREFIX` error as the generic `SendCommand` arm (`connection.rs:1088-1092`)★ · it does not just drop the oneshot (then the caller would see, instead of 「안 나갔다」, the wording that comes out of RecvError) (it is not `SENT_OUTCOME_UNKNOWN` `:1306` — that wording is attached by the disconnect drain to a pending that has already gone out `:657`, and here nothing went out). The only one who writes the socket is the connection task, so the order is fixed and on the shell side one of the two halves cannot vanish alone — if the subscription write fails, the refresh does not go out, and if the refresh write fails, the ⟳ request comes back as a failure like other forwarded commands. ★Amendment 14 — Stage 8 implementation correction (§3 #93): even if the subscription write fails, the connection task's loop does not end on the spot★ — `send_request` only returns the error as that refresh's `reply` and does not leave the loop, and if the socket is really dead, the **next** `select` sees the disconnect by itself (`connection.rs` — the contract is that the next select sees Disconnected; this failure itself does not break the loop). ★On a superseded socket (one where the interest state already looks at a newer socket — `sync(.., true)` is `None`), the subscription frame is omitted and only the refresh frame goes out on that socket like an ordinary command★ — the subscription of that socket is sent when the new socket opens (「새 소켓마다 관심을 다시 보낸다」 above). ★A remaining gap — if the daemon refuses that subscription (receiving line saturated)★: the refresh `Ack` still arrives and `pending` is resolved, but that company's value does not come. If an event of **another** subscribed company arrives on that connection, its `subscribed` reveals the divergence and the reconciliation resends and heals it (「대조」 above). If no other company emits events on that connection, it stays as is until ⟳ again · a layout change that changes interest · a new socket (§6 #25). ★There is no separately inserted forced nudge★ — in the old shape (amendment 6: insert the forced nudge first with `try_enqueue` and ⟳ after with `send_command`), when the command channel fills, only the forced nudge is dropped (`daemon_client/mod.rs:871-881`) while the refresh goes out as is (`:756-774`), so a refresh with no subscription ran and ⟳ showed nothing (amendment 6 re-review codex blind FIX). The answer of ⟳ resolves only `pending` (§1-8).
- **The path by which the webview receives values (웹뷰가 값을 받는 길) (amendment 6):** ① broadcast — the shell delivers to the windows that have a usage slot (`usage_windows` — in any tab) (「방송 전달」 below). There is no harm if a hidden window receives it — interest is computed separately. ② initial and hand-off — the new command `#[tauri::command] get_usage_snapshot() -> UsageSnapshotReply{socket_epoch: u64, snapshots: Vec<UsageLimitSnapshot>}` (= `snapshot_for_webview` · `socket_epoch` = the current socket marker, 0 if none) — called **after** the webview has hooked up broadcast reception (§1-8 「값 받기」). This is the path that keeps a new popout · a refreshed page · a newly placed usage slot from staying empty until the next broadcast (up to 15 minutes). ★A refresh does not touch interest★ — the layout lives in the shell and crosses the refresh, so interest, `sent` and the daemon subscription stay as they are, and the page that comes back up only pulls the cache again (§1-9 #12).
- **One serial path (한 직렬 경로):** the one who writes `UsageSubscribe` to the socket is a single connection task (socket writes are only by the connection task — `connection.rs:207-209`). The side that changes interest (layout recompute · hide · shrink timer) puts in only one nudge `ConnectionCommand::UsageInterest{socket}` that does not carry the set, through `try_enqueue` (`daemon_client/mod.rs:871-882`) (⟳ is not a nudge — at the place where the refresh frame is written, `sync(.., force=true)` runs together, 「⟳」 above · amendment 7), and when the connection task takes it out it reads **that moment's** interest with `sync` and sends it — read order = send order, so the last message is always the latest interest. ★A shape in which the caller reads the set inside the lock and does `send_fire_and_forget` outside the lock lets the old set arrive last if two changes cross between the read and the send (§3 #74)★. Every method the connection task calls carries its own `socket_epoch` and does nothing if it is not the current marker (for `on_socket_open`: if its marker is not greater than the largest marker seen) — so that a task pushed out by succession (「소켓 표식」 below) does not touch the new socket's `sent` and cache (the same reason as the endpoint-close guard `lifecycle.rs:312-322`). A nudge also carries the ADR-0195 socket marker and is dropped if it belongs to an old socket (`reject_foreign_command` `connection.rs:1341`). A nudge is a no-op if the endpoint is closed (`daemon_client/mod.rs:871-878` — it is sent again at the new socket · for the gap before `cmd_tx` is saved, the nudge right after saving fills it, 「새 소켓」 below). If `Defer`, the caller launches one task that sleeps 1.5 seconds on the client runtime and then, if `deferral_elapsed(세대)` is `true`, puts in one nudge.
- **Window destruction (창 소멸):** `Destroyed` of `on_window_event` (`src-tauri/src/lib.rs:190-205` — only popup labels are destroyed) → `cleanup_popup_window` recomputes inside the same lock in which it erased the window from the model (「재계산 자리」 ② above) → if it shrinks, `Defer`. ★The same recompute also erases the destroyed label from `hidden` (amendment 7)★ — a label is not reused (`commands/popout.rs:40-49`), so if left, `hidden` would leak one cell for every popout that was closed while hidden. main is not destroyed since close = hide (`lib.rs:178-183`) — 「숨김」 below. ★Amendment 14 — Stage 8 implementation correction (§3 #90): the only path that erases the hidden marker is window destruction ([`UsageInterest::forget_window`])★ — an ordinary `recompute` (every other place where layout or visibility changes) does not touch `hidden`. Because, in the gap of phase B of moving a slot to another window (open the OS window first, release the lock, and add to the model in phase C), if the tray hidden marker were erased, a hidden popout could be counted as visible during that gap — if `recompute` erases the marker, this gap arises, and if only `forget_window` erases it, it does not.
- **Hide (숨김) = the shell removes interest:** hiding to the tray is done by the shell itself — `visibilitychange` has no precedent in `src/` (`rg visibilitychange src/` → 0 lines). That path is one (ADR-0229 — main X `lib.rs:178-183` · boot `--hidden` `:167-169` · tray left click, the two commands and relaunch all go through the two functions of `tray::actions`): `hide_main_ui` (`src-tauri/src/tray/actions.rs:77-84`) for each window it hides (`core::ui_windows` — main + popouts) · `show_main_ui` (`:62-72`) for each window it shows takes the ViewManager lock briefly and `set_visible(label, false|true, mgr)` → the same `action` as above (if it shrinks, 1.5-second `Defer` · if it grows, nudge at once). ★What that lock wraps is only the `set_visible` state update (amendment 7)★ — the OS window calls `w.hide()`/`w.show()` are placed **outside** the lock: those two functions also run on the command worker thread (`src-tauri/src/commands/tray.rs`) · it is the same danger as keeping the window host port outside the lock (what `src-tauri/src/layout/apply.rs:90-99` named for `WindowHost`). The slot of a hidden window stays in the layout and the shell removes it only from interest — when shown again the shell sends it back. ★Minimizing does not count as hiding — user decision 2026-09-27 (§2)★ — a minimized window is a visible window, so the query continues (minimizing is per window — ADR-0229 decision 1 · the shell does not track minimizing). 「창 없으면 구독 해제」 (user proposal 2026-09-27) is borne by this path and window destruction together.
- **Socket marker (소켓 표식 · `socket_epoch`) — on every usage payload going to the webview (amendment 3 · the name is amendment 4 — everywhere in this path `socket_epoch`/`socketEpoch`, a bare `epoch` is forbidden):** the broadcast `{labels, socket_epoch, snapshot}` · the cache pull return `{socket_epoch, snapshots}` (amendment 6) carry the shell's socket marker (ADR-0195 `socket_epoch` — `open_socket_if_current` draws it fresh for every socket and the numberer is never rewound `lifecycle.rs:78-81`·`:298-310` · 0 = no socket). The frontend forgets the revision only when this marker grows (the only forgetting rule — §1-8) · the shell cache is also bound to this marker (`on_socket_open` clears it). ★The reason it is needed = generation succession Connected→Connected★ — when a new generation pushes out the old one, the entry's `Connecting` has no firing (`daemon_client/mod.rs:395-398`) and the pushed-out task does not emit `Down` (`connection.rs:702-709`·`:715-723`), so the webview meets the new socket without ever leaving `connected` — the old 「`connected` 를 떠날 때 잊기」 (removed in amendment 4 — §1-8) could not see this. If beyond that new socket is a restarted daemon, revision starts from 0 so the first snapshot is discarded, and if there is a stored refusal, no query goes out either, so there is no way to fill it in (codex finding — §6 #22). ★The size comparison has meaning only inside the lifetime of one shell process★ — since the numberer is never rewound, size is simply socket order (it differs from the incarnation marker of ADR-0163 — that one is random, so only match/mismatch). The 「프로세스 밖으로 나가지 않는다(디스크에도 wire 에도)」 at the head of `lifecycle.rs:72-76` is fixed in Stage 6 to 「웹뷰 사용량 payload 에는 실린다 — 데몬 wire·디스크엔 없다」.
- **Interest is sent again at every new socket (새 소켓마다 관심을 다시 보낸다):** **after** the connection task opens the endpoint (first connection `connection.rs:281` · reconnection `:841` — `open_socket_if_current` is the only place that opens the endpoint) and **before** `main_loop` reads the command channel, if `on_socket_open(socket_epoch)` (marker = the value received when the endpoint was opened) gives interest, it sends one `UsageSubscribe` directly to the socket (`sent := interest`). A nudge that gets in between is handled after this in the command channel and compares the interest of that moment with `sent`, so the last message = the latest interest. ★The gap before `cmd_tx` is saved (amendment 4)★: in the first connection and generation succession, even if the endpoint opens, `cmd_tx` is saved by the caller as `store_cmd_if_current` after `ready_rx.await` (`daemon_client/mod.rs:538-544` · `lifecycle.rs:292-294`·`:335-341` · succession is cleared by `bump_and_capture` `lifecycle.rs:147`), so in between `current_cmd_tx()` is `None` and the nudge of a layout recompute becomes a no-op — if `on_socket_open` has already read the interest, that change goes nowhere. → Right after `store_cmd_if_current` succeeds, the shell puts in one `UsageInterest{socket}` nudge by itself — since `sync` compares with `sent`, if nothing got into the gap, nothing is sent (idempotent). When the socket ends (the place where `connected_lifetime` closes the endpoint `connection.rs:645` — regardless of why it ended), `on_socket_lost(socket_epoch)`. ★This differs from the old eager resubscribe (removed by ADR-0046 — `output_router.rs:109`)★ — that one re-unrolled FromOldest replay per window and broke the seq monotonicity, while this set is a whole replacement so sending it back is idempotent. ★It also does not run into ADR-0164 (the amendment of the reconnection clause of ADR-0046 decision 2), which rejected resubscription triggered by a socket transition (amendment 3)★ — the reason for that rejection was a structural refusal storm in which the full `Subscribe` was fired at the **empty registry** of a restarted daemon and everything was refused (the first item of that ADR 「맥락」·「거부한 대안」). `UsageSubscribe` has no existence premise — the company word is resolved by the static querier registry, not the agent registry (§1-6 key normalization), and there is neither an answer nor a refusal, so even a restarted daemon has nothing to refuse on the grounds of existence. This is a stronger basis than idempotence. ★It also differs from ADR-0195 (keeping commands while the endpoint is closed)★ — it does not pile up commands but rebuilds one sheet from state (§3 #61 — the review judged it valid).
- **Broadcast delivery (방송 전달) — one new hop (Stage 6):** ★the usage arm stands not inside `emit_broadcast` but at its call site (`daemon_client/connection.rs:1015`) (amendment 4)★ — `emit_broadcast` (`:1228-1266`) is a synchronous function that receives only `(events, ev)`, so it has no interest state, socket marker or socket exit, and the call site is inside the connection task that holds `sink`·`socket_epoch`. That site first splits `UsageLimitsUpdated{snapshot}` (the remaining variants go on as is to `emit_broadcast`) → `on_snapshot(socket_epoch, snapshot, subscribed, now)` (the connection task's socket marker is passed along) → one method `DaemonEvents::usage_limits_updated(&UsageLimitSnapshot, labels: &[String], socket_epoch: u64)` (`daemon_client/events.rs:71-90`) on the notification port → the production `TauriEmitter` (`:104-141`) emits `{labels, socket_epoch, snapshot}` with `"usage-limits-updated"` (the event name is owned by the adapter — `:20`) · if `resend`, at the same place one `UsageSubscribe{resend 집합}` sheet with `sink` (the interest the reconciliation saw as different — 「대조」 above · amendment 6). ★The targets are chosen by the payload's `labels` (= the windows that have a usage slot — amendment 6)★ — the webview receives only when its own label is in it (`window:tabs-updated` precedent `commands/layout.rs:29-30` — it does not rely on the target decision of Tauri `emit_to`). A window without a usage slot has no reason to receive (§3 #63). The same method on the harness `NoDaemonEvents` (`:157-164`) and the test `RecordingEvents` (`daemon_client/tests.rs:3697`). ★If the arm is missing there is no signal★ — if the call site's arm is absent, that variant falls to the `_ => {}` of `emit_broadcast` and the compiler cannot catch it, so 「방송 JSON 한 줄 → 알림 한 번(+ labels · `socket_epoch`)」 is measured with a recording fake (§4 shell). The 「여기 있는 여섯 가지가 연결 태스크가 하는 emit 전부 — 넓히지 말 것」 (`events.rs:68-70`) at the port head is fixed to seven — it is the same kind that moves one daemon broadcast 1:1, so it does not run into that warning (the port becoming a substitute for AppHandle): reconciliation and resend are outside the port (the call site and the pure module), and the port method receives only computed values (§6 #23 — review confirmed).
- Generated artifacts: regenerate `src-tauri/bindings/` (CI sync gate).

### 1-8. frontend

- **Surface:** two on `agentClient` (the `api/agentClient.ts:183-188` form) — `refreshUsageLimits(vendor)` → `ProtocolClient.sendCommand` (the `listPresets` `protocolClient.ts:973-974` form · the answer is the existing `Ack`, so the `Ack` branch `:631-632` of `handleEvent` releases the wait — no new answer branch, amendment 6) · ★`getUsageSnapshot()` (amendment 6)★ → it is a shell command, so one method on the transport seam's `Transport` (`requestReplay` `api/transport.ts:74` · the `tauriTransport.ts:458-468` form — `TauriTransport` = `invoke('get_usage_snapshot')` · `WsTransport` without a shell = `{socketEpoch: 0, snapshots: []}`). ★The store does not call `invoke` directly★ — components and stores depend only on `agentClient` (ADR-0011), and the transport is held by the transport seam (ADR-0036). ★There is no `getUsageLimits` (amendment 2)★ — values come by subscription.
- **Receiving values (값 받기) (amendment 6 — replaces the old 「관심 보고」 (`useUsageInterest` · interest reporting to the shell) · §3 #78):** the webview neither computes nor reports interest — the shell computes interest from the layout (§1-7 「관심」). The webview does two things. ① receive broadcasts and `merge` (「방송 수신」 below). ② ★subscribe first, pull later★ (the same order as `WindowLayout.tsx:58-60` — reversal is stopped by revision) — `merge` `agentClient.getUsageSnapshot()` (shell cache · no daemon round trip · returns `{socketEpoch, snapshots}`). Pull triggers = ⓐ once **right after** `store/eventBus.ts` hooks up usage broadcast (`onUsageLimitsUpdated` — next to `onPresetListUpdated` `:174-179`) · ⓑ when `UsageSlot` mounts. ★Why ⓐ is needed★ — `initEventBus` runs at boot **after** `await bootstrapDaemonIfNeeded()` (`App.tsx:43-44`) → if a slot mounts before that, the pull of ⓑ goes out before the hookup and may miss a broadcast in between, and ⓐ pulls again after the hookup to cover it. For mounts after the hookup, ⓑ alone stands the order. With several slots there are several pulls, but `merge` tells them apart. Failure is a bounded retry (`retryAsync` `util/retryInvoke.ts:34-59` — the value is kept). ★Amendment 14 — Stage 8 implementation decision (§3 #97, a deviation from this TRD): the third trigger ⓒ = right after a reconnection★ — the `resyncAfterReconnect` of `store/eventBus.ts` (only on the reconnection *re*-transition, excluding the first connection) also calls `usageStore.pull()`. This TRD originally set no reconnection pull (on the premise that the loss of broadcasts during a disconnect is filled by the first snapshot of the new socket — §1-9 #4). ★Main decision (2026-09-29 — applying 7a review codex FIX)★: it covers together the remainder where, after the first pull right after startup failed (`retryAsync` retries exhausted), there is no trigger at all afterwards (doc-aware finding #2), and the remainder where, as in generation succession, the socket marker does not change yet that window missed broadcasts while disconnected — even if the same snapshot is received again, the `merge` merge rule (revision comparison) filters it out and it does no harm (idempotent). ⟳ = ★one global button on the small display (amendment 15 — §3 #102)★ (★amendment 19 — the popup also gets a per-company ⟳ · the same press rule applied to that one company (press rule = main proposal — applying the global ⟳ rule mutatis mutandis) · §3 #106 ②★) · ★press ignored (user decision 2026-09-29 — 「조회가 도는동안 받기전까지 다시 누르면 무시」): if **even one** of the target companies has a query in progress, the press is ignored as a whole (★amendment 20 — there is still no new query, and only the ⟳ rotation restarts from the beginning · §3 #107 ②★) — at worst it is blocked until the query deadline (Claude 15 seconds · Codex 30 seconds) · the daemon also joins the in-progress query per company★ · ★pressing ⟳ with the popup open does not close the popup (the ⟳ row counts as inside the popup) · Esc on ⟳ closes the popup but does not move focus★ · `refreshUsageLimits` for each company turned on (~~the 30-second interval is applied by the daemon~~ amendment 15 — no interval, an in-progress query is joined · the shell connection task sends the subscription back on the same socket right before that frame — §1-7 「⟳」, amendment 7). Time flow (age · remaining time · "N분 전") runs locally from the received `age_secs` + `performance.now()` at the moment of receipt — even with no poll, it is drawn with one per-minute tick. A directly connected `WsTransport` has no shell, so the pull is empty and there is no interest computation — production is fixed to `TauriTransport` (ADR-0036), so it is out of scope.
- **Broadcast reception (방송 수신) (Stage 7 — the same path as the existing daemon broadcast):** one `listen('usage-limits-updated')` on `TauriTransport.registerListeners` (the `preset-list-updated` `api/tauriTransport.ts:236-243` form) — only when its own window label is in `labels` of the payload `{labels, socket_epoch, snapshot}` (the `WindowLayout.tsx:71-73` form), pass it on as the control event `{UsageLimitsUpdated:{snapshot}}` of `messageCb`, accompanied by the shell's `socket_epoch` (amendment 3 · the wire's `subscribed` is used by the shell only for reconciliation and is not carried in the webview payload — amendment 6) · the `UsageLimitsUpdated` branch on `ProtocolClient.handleEvent` (the `PresetListUpdated` `protocolClient.ts:697-701` form) + `onUsageLimitsUpdated(cb: (snapshot, socketEpoch) => void)` (the `onPresetListUpdated` `:1014-1019` form) · `store/eventBus.ts` hooks it up to `usageStore` (the `onPresetListUpdated` `:174-179` form — subscription lifetime is owned by eventBus).
- **Store:** new `store/usageStore.ts` (zustand) — the last snapshot per company + its `revision` + the received `performance.now()` + the current `socketEpoch` (amendment 3) + `pending` (⟳ wait — shared among slots inside the webview). Amendment 6 — no per-slot interest or report number is kept (interest is the shell's share — §1-7).
  - ★`socketEpoch` is not an incarnation marker (ADR-0163 `epoch`)★ — size comparison holds only because it is a counter that is never rewound inside the shell process (`lifecycle.rs:78-81` · a 2^64 wrap is unreachable). 0 = no socket. The code attaches the `// ADR-0195` anchor to this comparison place.
  - ★Receiving = one function (`merge`) — shared by broadcast and shell cache pull (the ⟳ answer has no value — below)★: ★first `socketEpoch` (amendment 3 · amendment 4 — the only forgetting rule)★: if the payload's `socketEpoch` is greater than what it holds, hold it, forget all per-company revisions (values kept — R22), and then receive · if smaller, discard (it belongs to a pushed-out socket) · `0` (no socket in the shell — in that case the pull return is empty) does not change `socketEpoch`. ★⟳ answer = the existing `Ack` — resolves only `pending` (amendment 5 · amendment 6: the answer has no snapshot itself)★. The value comes by broadcast: if it is ⟳ of a visible slot, that company is in the shell's interest, the shell connection task sends the subscription back on the same socket right before the ⟳ frame (§1-7 「⟳」 — amendment 7: the two are one action, so one of them cannot vanish alone), and a company newly added to the connection's set receives the first snapshot (§1-4). The ⟳ answer comes through the generic path `forward_daemon_command`, on which the shell does not carry a marker, but since it carries no value, there is no place for a C2-type remainder (a value without a marker being accepted under an old marker) to arise. Then revision: if `snapshot.revision < 들고 있는 revision`, discard · if greater, receive · ★if equal, receive the one that arrived late (amendment 3)★ — it is a copy of the same state on a newer time basis: since the shell corrects the relative cells when handing it off (§1-7 「핸드오프 보정」), the difference in reference time between the two copies is only the delivery delay inside the shell. The two paths (Tauri emit · pull invoke return — the ⟳ answer's path carries no value) do not guarantee order with respect to each other.
  - There is no webview-side recovery of divergence (amendment 6 — the old 「관심 밖 방송 → 버리고 재보고」 was removed together with the report · §3 #64 record) — a broadcast for a company that is not turned on is also held in the store, and only drawing is filtered to the companies that are turned on. Daemon-side divergence is caught by the shell's `subscribed` reconciliation (§1-7 「대조」).
  - ★The only time the revision is forgotten is when `merge` accepts a **greater** `socketEpoch` — the connection leaving `connected` does not touch the revision floor (amendment 4 — the second rule 「`connected` 를 떠나면 잊기」 of amendment 3 removed)★. A daemon restart counts revision from 0 again, but that is always a new socket so the rule above covers it — forgetting and the first payload of the new socket happen inside one `merge` and so do not rely on event order, and it also covers generation succession in which the connection state does not leave `connected` (§1-7 「소켓 표식」). ★The old basis 「잊기만 하므로 더 받게만 만든다 — 1차와 어긋나도 해가 없다」 was wrong (codex re-review)★: the disconnect transition arrives on a different path from the usage payload (`daemon-connection-state` — `tauriTransport.ts:256-260` → `applyConnectionState` `:111`), so if, **after** receiving the payload (rev N) with the new socket marker, the transition of an earlier disconnect arrives late and erases the floor, a smaller-revision payload (< N — the two paths do not guarantee order with respect to each other) that arrives late on the same socket would be accepted and the screen would rewind to an old snapshot. All a disconnect transition does is release ⟳ waits — `ProtocolClient` refuses all waiting requests in that transition (`protocolClient.ts:177-181`) so `pending` is released, and the ⟳ answer of the old daemon does not come after that. Values and revision stay as they are. ★The old basis 「새 데몬의 첫 스냅숏이 버려져도 곧바로 조회해 몇 초 안에 메워진다」 is not used★ — if there is a stored refusal, the new daemon does not query (codex finding — §6 #22).
- **Pure `usageFormat.ts` (R10·D17):** `left = clamp(100 − used, 0, 100)` · **visible number = `Math.floor(left)`** · color by the visible number — `> 50` ok · `20–50` warn · `< 20` danger ~~+ ⚠~~ (amendment 16 — §3 #103) · absent → gray "—" (R21) · age = `age_secs + (performance.now() − 받은 시각)/1000` · `STALE_AFTER_SECS = 1800` (R32) · remaining time = `resets_at` − local wall clock (R29).
- **Component:** new `components/slot/UsageSlot.tsx`.
  - Width stage (R3·D16): the actual width by ResizeObserver (amendment 18 — measured width = slot width − ⟳ slot − margin · §3 #104), and the natural width of each stage is measured in a hidden render (`visibility:hidden`). The widest stage that fits · ~~the remaining time is~~ the reset time is only in stages 1 and 2 (amendment 16 — §3 #103 ④) · if narrower than stage 3, `text-overflow: ellipsis` — no px constant. ~~★Amendment 14 — 7b review follow-up (§3 #99): stage 2 looks at **height** as well as width★ — if the hidden-render height of stage 2 (`s2h`) exceeds the slot root height (`rootH`, including inner padding), it falls to stage 3 even if the width fits (`pickStage` — `UsageSlot.tsx`). The `!` badge of R31 appears at every width stage, but if stage 2 is cut off by a line wrap, the badge may vanish from the cut line — stage 3 (the narrowest stage where only numbers remain) never wraps, so the badge is always visible.~~ ★Amendment 19 (user decision 2026-09-30 · §3 #106 ①): height does not change the stage★ — it is chosen by width only, and the slot root cuts off the bottom for missing height (the badge on a cut line is also hidden). ★Amendment 20 (§3 #107 ⑤): the hidden measurement copy no longer counts the updating marker — there is no marker · and there is no stage-3 empty space either★.
  - Bar = `role="meter"` + `aria-valuenow` (visible number) · `aria-valuetext` (D14). Values and times are DOM text (R27) + `data-usage-vendor`/`data-usage-window` (for cdp QA). "갱신 중" = `pending` or the snapshot's `in_flight` (a query opened by the scheduler also reaches us by broadcast at its start — §1-4) — the value stays the previous one. ★Amendment 20 (user decision 2026-10-01 · §3 #107): the visible indication of 「갱신 중」 is the rotation of the ⟳ button — there is no per-company marker (next to the small-display icon · the popup header text). The decision (`pending` ∨ `in_flight`) is as is, and the `aria-busy`·`data-usage-refreshing` of ⟳ and the summary button's accessible name carry it★.
  - Expiry: `expired` (daemon latch) or `resets_at ≤ 지금` → hide the % and countdown and show "리셋됨 — 갱신 대기" (R32). Stale → the small display **dims only the number** (no age wording) · "N분 전" only in the popup (★amendment 19 — once on the company header · amber when over 30 minutes · §3 #106 ②·④★) · one per-minute tick. The five state wordings (R31) go through `t()` — even for a failure or refusal, the value held is drawn (R22 · `Unavailable` has no value, so a gray "—").
  - Status badge (R31 — user decision 2026-09-27): in the five non-normal states (including `Unavailable` — 4-0), the small display shows, instead of wording, **one `!` badge** next to the ~~company name~~ company icon (amendment 16) — at every width stage (including the stage where only numbers remain, e.g. 「(Claude 아이콘)! 62·41」), attached right next to the ~~name~~ icon (outside the ellipsis) so it is not cut off by ellipsis. ~~The glyph does not overlap with `⚠` (< 20%, R10).~~ (⚠ removed — amendment 16) The badge element's `aria-label`·`title` = the one-line wording of that state (hover display + the DOM text the LLM reads — R27). The same wording is put on the top line of the popup (canonical — touch has no hover). The wordings (the five states and "N분 전") are all `t(key)` + value insertion (R28 · ADR-0069), and the time is `Intl` — the daemon, the wire and the LLM bus read path carry only state code + number + detail (e.g. `Failed{next_attempt_in_secs, detail}`), and do not carry display strings to be translated.
  - ★State detail (amendment 2 — failure tracing)★: one-line wording = state wording (`t`) + detail — if there is `upstream`, 「<회사> 응답: <upstream>」 (the prefix is `t` · the raw text is inserted **as is, untranslated** · if there is `code`, in parentheses after the prefix), otherwise 「(<kind>)」 — + numbers such as the next attempt. Example: 「조회 실패 — Claude 응답: rate_limits_available: true, rate_limits: null · 다음 시도 14:05」 · 「조회 실패 (timeout) · 다음 시도 14:05」. The badge `aria-label`·`title` and the top line of the popup use the same sentence (R27 — the LLM also reads it from the DOM). The exact sentence shape is Stage 7.
  - Popup (팝업) (R5–R7·D8 — ★only companies that are turned on★: a company that is turned off is nowhere in the small display, popup or badge, and is also removed from interest · user confirmed 2026-09-27): the summary area is `<button>` (click · Enter · Space — D14) · `position: fixed` + `clampMenuPosition` (`SlotContextMenu.tsx:33`) · closes with Esc or an outside click · absolute reset time per window (`Intl.DateTimeFormat` · ★amendment 19 — an hourglass icon instead of the word, §3 #106 ③★) · ~~age always shown per value~~ ★amendment 19 — the age is shown once on the company header 「플랜: max · 1분 전 ⟳」 (§3 #106 ②)★ · plan (only when present) · per-model windows · 「사용량 페이지 ↗」 (`@tauri-apps/plugin-opener` `openUrl` — `package.json:22`, destination explained with `title`) · ~~⟳ (visible refusal = 「거절됨 — N분 뒤」 + ⟳ disabled · a refusal while a value has arrived and shows as `Ready` = ⟳ quietly answers from cache — no query goes out · no indication, §3 #88 ⓑ C). No toggle. Both companies off → one guidance line (R4).~~ ★Amendment 15 (user decision 2026-09-29 · §3 #102)★: ~~there is no ⟳ in the popup (refresh is the global ⟳ of the small display — the disable-on-refusal and quiet-cache-answer rules above apply to that button as is)~~ ★amendment 19 (user decision 2026-09-30 · §3 #106 ② · ADR-0259): the popup has one ⟳ per company (next to the age on the header — that company only) · the global ⟳ of the small display stays · the disable-on-refusal and quiet-cache-answer rules apply equally to both buttons (press rule = main proposal — applying the global ⟳ rule mutatis mutandis) · opening the popup does not query★ · the bottom on/off row 「슬롯에 표시: ☑ Claude ☑ Codex」 (wraps if companies grow · execution = ~~whole replacement of `useViewStore.setSlotContent`~~ `viewStore.setUsageSlot` → webview Tauri command `set_usage_slot` — only its own cell as an absolute `!current[field]` (amendment 17 — main decision 2026-09-30 · §3 #102 ⑤) — moved here from 「메뉴」 below) · details only for companies turned on · both companies off → 「클릭해서 표시할 항목 고르기」 on the small display (R4) · only the on/off row in the popup. An absolute reset time, on a day that is not today, is date + time without a weekday (the same rule as the small display — §3 #102). ★Amendment 20 (user decision 2026-10-01 · ADR-0261): the window rows are **one table** across both company cells — columns = name | bar | % | reset time | remaining time (§3 #108) · there is no 「갱신 중」 text at the end of the company header — that company's ⟳ spins (§3 #107)★.
- **Menu (메뉴) (R8):** ★amendment 15 (user decision 2026-09-29 · §3 #102): there is no usage menu item★ — the items registered with `registerSlotMenu('usage', …)` are 0 and only the generic slot menu remains. The commands `usageSlot.refresh`·`usageSlot.toggleClaude`·`usageSlot.toggleCodex` remain as the LLM path (「LLM-우선 제어」 · a human's toggle is the on/off row of 「팝업」 above). The `'usage'` of `'*'` hideOn (`slotContentCommands.ts`) and the `slot.fill.usage` of the child of the empty slot 「새 콘텐츠」 stay as they are.
  - The old menu design (☑ = `SlotMenuItem.checked` · ⟳ disabled = `enabled(ctx)` · the ctx `usageRefreshable` that the assembly site LayoutLeaf subscribed to and carried) has no consumer — `usageRefreshable` and LayoutLeaf's usage-store subscription were removed in `910c214`, and only the `checked`·`enabled` cells and the 「메뉴 상태는 조립처가 ctx 로 싣는다」 rule remain (no state is carried now). The history (including undoing the `Command.when` extension) and open items = §3 #21·#98 · ADR-0252 · ADR-0258 「영향」.

The five section rules (`:8`) stay as they are.

### 1-9. Scenarios for startup, first connection, reconnection, popout, all hidden, shutdown, refresh (there is no new connection-lifetime message — a subscription is tied to the connection and a disconnect = an empty set)

| # | Scenario | Action | Anchor · test |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | The first subscription or request right after daemon startup | ★Removed by ADR-0284 (2026-10-08)★ — there is no refusal file, so a new daemon starts with no refusal deadline (first subscription = the book's current state · the scheduler may query). What follows is the design before removal — ~~the refusal file is read synchronously inside assembly (`lib.rs:609` → `:264`) and the service is built in that state → it is before `run_accept_loop` (`:738`). The first snapshot of the first subscription, the scheduler and the first request all cannot get past a stored refusal~~ | ~~Test: assemble with a store that holds a future item → first subscription = one snapshot `Rejected` (detail `rate_limited` — amendment 3 · `subscribed` = that set — amendment 6) · scheduler queries 0 · `refresh` = cache + `Rejected`~~ (that test was deleted by ADR-0284) |
| 2 | App startup (the daemon does not exist yet) | The shell computes interest from its own layout regardless of the connection (boot default = one empty slot `layout/manager.rs:146-161` → interest ∅ · once layout persistence stands, from the restored layout — #7). When a usage slot is placed, recompute → since it is not connected, the nudge is a no-op (endpoint closed `daemon_client/mod.rs:871-878`) and only holds the interest → when the first socket opens (`connection.rs:281`) `on_socket_open` sends one sheet → the daemon issues the first snapshot per company (`subscribed` carried) + since there is no reference point, queries at once. ★A recompute that falls after the endpoint opens and before `cmd_tx` is saved (`store_cmd_if_current` after `ready_rx.await` — `daemon_client/mod.rs:538-544`) is also a no-op nudge → right after the save the shell puts in one nudge by itself to fill it (amendment 4 — §1-7 「새 소켓」)★. The webview pulls the shell cache after hooking up broadcast (an empty return if not connected — the value comes with the first broadcast) | Shell `lib_unit`: recompute while disconnected → send 0 · connect → one interest sheet · recompute in the gap before `cmd_tx` is saved → sent after the save · vitest: pull 1 after broadcast hookup |
| 3 | First subscription on an empty cache | First snapshot (no value) → the scheduler starts a query (`in_flight` broadcast) → completion broadcast about 1 second later (Claude 1.2–1.4 seconds · Codex 0.74–0.94 seconds measured). Two companies at once → companies do not wait for each other · if ⟳ and the bus overlap on the same company, they join (one query per company) | Test: subscribe two companies → queries 1 per company · even if Codex is a blocked fake, the Claude broadcast is not late |
| 4 | The client stays and the daemon restarts | A waiting ⟳ errors out by the disconnect drain (`connection.rs:649-656` · not a permanent hang — `src-tauri/tests/daemon_client_pending.rs`) → the previous value is kept. The shell clears the cache when it loses the socket (`sent := ∅`) and sends interest again on the new socket (`:841`) → the new daemon = first snapshot + query (~~if there is a stored refusal, no query — #1. The refusal file remains (#1).~~ removed by ADR-0284, 2026-10-08 — the old daemon's refusal deadline does not follow). ★The revision starts again from 0★ — the frontend forgets at the first payload that carries the new `socketEpoch` (the only forgetting rule — §1-8 store · it does not forget by leaving `connected`, amendment 4). ★Generation succession (Connected→Connected — §1-7 「소켓 표식」) is the same★ — even with no connection-state transition, the socket marker grows and it forgets, the shell cache is cleared by `on_socket_open`, and the late broadcast, `on_socket_lost` and `on_socket_open` of the pushed-out task are discarded because the marker differs (is smaller) | Shell: reconnection → one interest sheet · cache cleared · succession = broadcast, socket loss and socket open with an old marker ignored · vitest: a smaller revision accepted after a new socket marker · value kept · accepted even when only the marker grows with no connection-state transition · the revision is not forgotten by a disconnect transition alone |
| 5 | Startup race | Boot auto-restore is off by default (`lib.rs:725-731` — no-op). Even if the user starts an agent right away, the query is a separate process and a separate Job — no special handling | — |
| 6 | Daemon shutdown during a query | Both the query and the scheduler are detached threads, so the runtime drop does not wait, and as the Job closes the child tree dies · the scheduler ends by itself on the service drop (§1-4) | Test: even if the fake querier is blocked, the service drop and runtime shutdown finish at once · service drop → the scheduler thread ends (★even with a blocked fake query in progress — the query thread holds only `Weak`, amendment 3★) |
| 7 | Layout persistence (future) | Even if the shell computes interest from the restored layout immediately at boot, #1–#3 still cover it (amendment 6 — it does not wait for the webview to come up) | — |
| 8 | Broadcasts while disconnected or before attaching | Cannot be received — a subscription is tied to the connection and is erased when it breaks (`on_disconnect`). The interest resend on the new socket → the first snapshot fills it (#4) | Daemon: connection broken → registry cell erased · other connections unaffected |
| 9 | Popout (a tab into a new window) | ★Amendment 14 — 6c review follow-up correction (§3 #100): `move_slot_to_window` phase A does not emit `Defer`★ — phase A that detaches the slot (inside the lock) only **copies the source content into a temporary View**, and that temporary View crosses phase B (outside the lock — building the new window's webview) in an owner-less state in which it enters no window's tabs. ★The source slot itself stays alive, and its content stays visible, until phase C (inside the lock — insert the temporary View into the target window + close the source slot)★ (`apply.rs:691-699` comment 「소스 콘텐츠는 소스 슬롯이 아직 살아 있어 계속 보인다」) — the temporary View is not a tab of any window to begin with, so it is never caught by `interest_from_layout` (iterating over windows), and the source slot stays in interest until it is closed in phase C. So in the recompute of phase A, interest does not shrink (it does not become a proper subset of `sent`), and `Defer` does not go out either — the frames going to the daemon are **0** across phases A to C (the moment of shrinking never exists). The new window enters `usage_windows` and the new webview pulls the shell cache after the broadcast hookup and draws at once (relative-cell correction · the value is exactly what the slot before the move was already subscribing to, so there is no new query either). When the popup is closed (`Destroyed` → `cleanup_popup_window`), recompute → if it shrinks, `Defer` | Shell: one move transaction (phases A to C) → send 0 (`lib_unit`) · pull return = cache (corrected) · popup destruction → removed from interest |
| 10 | All hidden (hidden to the tray · hidden tab · slot closed · both companies off) | Hiding to the tray: the shell sets a hidden marker per window and removes it from interest (`hide_main_ui` — §1-7 「숨김」) · the other three (tab switch · slot close · slot content set) are layout writes, so the shell recomputes on the spot (amendment 6) → interest becomes empty and 1.5 seconds later `UsageSubscribe{[]}` → the daemon union becomes empty and the scheduler does not query (「안 보이면 조회 안 함」). Even if that shrink nudge disappears (shell command channel saturated), the wait has already ended at the deadline, so when the next snapshot of a company about to drop out arrives (that company in `subscribed`), the reconciliation sends back the empty interest (§1-7 「대조」) — remaining cost ≤ one query. Pickup keeps editing the book but there is nowhere to send. When shown again (tray = `show_main_ui`), at the moment of growth → first snapshot (including values picked up in between) + a query if the deadline has passed | Daemon: union empty → `tick` queries 0 · no deadline · Shell: usage slots all dropped from the active tab → one empty set 1.5 seconds later · `hide_main_ui` → empty set 1.5 seconds later · `show_main_ui` → interest at once |
| 11 | Diverged subscription (reconciliation — amendment 6) | If the `subscribed` of a snapshot the daemon sent differs from the set the shell wants (`sent` if there is a pending shrink, otherwise the interest) → one sheet of that wanted set — if pending, send back `sent` and leave the wait as is (so a stale snapshot does not bring the shrink forward — amendment 7) · if there is no wait, one interest sheet + `sent := 관심` (§1-7 「대조」). One sheet per diverged snapshot — no suppression (amendment 8 · a same-set replacement in the daemon emits no event, so there is no feedback). It catches both a company that was turned on but is missing (triggered by another company's snapshot) and a company that was turned off but remains (triggered by that company's snapshot). The broadcast of a company about to drop out during the shrink delay is left as is, being `subscribed` = `sent`. ★A connection with no subscription at all gets no snapshot and has no trigger — ⟳ fixes it (§6 #25)★ | Shell: `subscribed` ≠ interest (no pending shrink) → `resend` = interest · during a pending shrink `subscribed` = `sent` → none · during a pending shrink ≠ `sent` → `resend` = `sent` · wait kept · equal → none · the same divergence twice → two sheets · shrink nudge lost → snapshot after the deadline → `resend` = interest |
| 12 | Webview refresh (amendment 6) | Does not touch interest — the layout lives in the shell and crosses the refresh (ADR-0035), so the shell's interest, `sent` and the daemon subscription stay as they are. The page that comes back up pulls the shell cache after the broadcast hookup and draws (`socketEpoch` is also received again by that return). The hole that the old load generation and order guards blocked (a late report or generation request from the page before the refresh) has no place to arise since there is no report path (§3 #78) | vitest: broadcast hookup → pull order · Shell: none (there is no shell-side event corresponding to a refresh — interest comes only from the layout) |

## 2. User decisions

**Confirmed (2026-09-27)**
- **Claude active query = A** (D2) — one line of `get_usage` on a short-lived CLI. B only as a swap alternative if A breaks (§1-3).
- **Cooldown = 15 minutes for both Claude and Codex (user decision 2026-09-27 — unified)** (D1·R15) — a named constant in each vendor file (§1-3).
- ~~**Request-type (pull) query** — a visible slot asks every minute per company, and the daemon queries only when the deadline has passed~~ → ★replaced (amendment 2)★ the subscription type below.
- **Subscription type (2026-09-27 — 「클라는 구독요청 해제 이벤트를 보내고 … 서버쪽에 관리가 들어간이상 15분 쿨타임도 서버쪽에서 관리하는게 맞아보임」 · 「클라가 창 없으면 구독 해제 이벤트 보내는걸로」)** — the client subscribes to the set of visible companies (a declarative set per connection · whole replacement — the user accepted this shape), and the daemon counts the cooldown and reset of subscribed companies and queries and emits by itself. Rejected = frontend 60-second poll + decision at request time (the old §3 #34 · §1-4 「태스크·타이머 없음」) — if the server already holds the cooldown and only the trigger is held by the client, polling and delay remain. Multi-client is out of scope (R29·D19).
- **Value changes are pushed out at once (2026-09-27 — 「이왕 빨리 전달할 수 있으면 즉각즉각이 맞지싶어」)** — in amendment 2, target = connections that subscribed to that company · trigger = whenever the revision rises (only pickup merged to 1 second). Rejected = apply at the next request (the old waiting map · pickup → screen ≤ 60 seconds). Peer basis = products that have a push source merge on arrival — t3code · Orca · Codex TUI · CodexMeter (`docs/research/usage-limit-display-ui-survey-2026-09-26.md:89-108`).
- **The popup follows the toggle (confirmed 2026-09-27)** — a company that is turned off is nowhere in the small display, popup or badge, and is also removed from interest. PRD R5 「전부 보인다」 = all detail items of a company that is turned on (D8).
- **A minimized window counts as a visible window (2026-09-27 — 「최소화할때 뭐 안한다는 기준서면 모든걸 다 그렇게 처리해야되잖아」)** — the query continues, and only hiding to the tray removes interest (§1-7 「숨김」). The cost = even if all windows are minimized, one query per company every 15 minutes (§6 #28 closed).
- **Interest is computed by the shell from its own layout (2026-09-27 — 「셸이 데이터만 들고있고 웹뷰는 그것기반으로 그려주는거잖아」)** — the sum of companies turned on by usage slots in the active tab of visible windows (not hidden to the tray · minimized = visible) (§1-7 「관심」). The webview → shell interest report and its ordering rules (amendments 3–5) are dropped — it was a second copy of the layout the shell already has (§3 #78).
- **The daemon carries `subscribed` and the shell reconciles (2026-09-27 — 「클로드만 와도 상태플래그 하나 더 와서 안맞으면 싱크」)** — every broadcast carries that connection's current company set · if it differs from the set the shell wants, interest is sent back (§1-7 「대조」). It replaces the old resend rules (a company outside `sent` · shrink healing).
- **⟳ = manual sync (2026-09-27 — 「체크온오프하고 동기화버튼 정도로 매칭」)** — the shell forcibly sends the subscription back and then passes ⟳ on (§1-7 「⟳」).
- **Rejected (user): resend everything every 30 seconds (2026-09-27 — 「사이드 이슈때문에 30초마다 매번 보내는걸 추가한다고?」)** — do not add a standing cost for one rare loss. The remaining gap (a connection with no subscription at all) is fixed by ⟳ (§6 #25).
- **Failure tracing = classification word + upstream raw text (2026-09-27 — 「오류 문구 원문이 있으면 보내야지 … 이상한거 다 섞어서 보내지 말고 타입은 보내야지」 · 「어쨋든 유저가 최대한 추적할 수 있도록」)** — `{kind, code, upstream}` per abnormal state · raw text only to the UI and LLM read paths (logging forbidden — main decision) · covering + 200 characters (§1-4).
- **No merge order rule between pickup and query** — the later write wins (§6 #17).
- **Do not distinguish the source of a value (pickup · query) (2026-09-28 — 「유저에게는 조회 줍기 구분하지말고 그냥 주고 데몬도 거기에 맞춰서 구성해」)** — a value is a value: one merge rule (what is carried overwrites, what is not carried is kept) · an observation holding a window value goes to `Ready` from any state · the source is not carried in the wire snapshot (§3 #88). Only the query gives what pickup cannot — account state (installed · login · no limit information · 429), not the accuracy of values. Rejected = distinguish the source so that only the query sets the state — the user does not need to know the source, and a new value coming means it is being updated (this came from asking why 「조회 실패」 remains although new pickup values keep coming).
- **`Unavailable` wording = 「사용량 정보 없음」 family (2026-09-27)** — the exact wording is Stage 7 (§6 #20).
- The pre-release legal and security confirmation (D3) applies to A as is — marker only.

**Confirmed (2026-09-29 — amendment 15 · §3 #102 · ADR-0258)**
- **Manual ⟳ = one global button on the small display** — right of the grid · at all three width stages · together with the companies turned on · hidden if both companies are off. ~~The per-company ⟳ in the popup and~~ the menu ⟳ are removed (PRD R12 · ★the popup's per-company ⟳ was revived by amendment 19 — ADR-0259 · the 2026-09-30 block below★).
- **On/off is the bottom row of the popup 「슬롯에 표시: ☑ Claude ☑ Codex」** — all usage items of the slot right-click menu are removed · the commands remain as the LLM path · popup details only for companies turned on (「끄면 사용량도 안보내고 좋네」) · if both are turned off, 「클릭해서 표시할 항목 고르기」 (PRD R4·R5·R7·R8).
- **The 30-second manual ⟳ interval is deleted (「다른데도 쿨타임 없으면 그대로 하자. 막히면 그네들이 알아서 적게 누르겠지」)** — joining an in-progress query and blocking during refusal (R24·D22) stay as they are (PRD D11).
- **Small-display reset time = the word 「리셋 00:58」 / 「resets 00:58」 · if the day is not today, the date 「리셋 10/1 01:48」 / 「resets Oct 1 01:48」** (PRD R2) · ★amendment 19 changed the word to an hourglass icon — the date rule is as is · ADR-0259★.

**Confirmed (2026-09-30 — amendment 17 · §3 #102 ⑤ · ADR-0258 decision 2)**
- **Accept the ADR-0063 exception — option A** — the user accepted that newly adding the webview IPC `set_usage_slot` (per-cell atomic merge) for the on/off write conflicts with 「슬롯 콘텐츠 변경은 `set_slot_content` 단일 경로」 of ADR-0063 and its rejected alternative 「variant별 전용 command」. Rejected = B (widening `set_slot_content` to partial cells — the user's reason = it costs more to implement · its breakdown (all call sites · one more review round) is the trade-off at the time of presentation — ADR-0258 「거부한 대안」).
- **Pressing the same cell again before it is reflected (echo) = keep as is** — the second click is effectively ignored (meaning = §3 #102 ⑤). The app-wide shared handling is a follow-up topic (`docs/tracking.md` T-36).

**Confirmed (2026-09-30 — amendment 19 · real-use feedback · §3 #105·#106 · ADR-0259·ADR-0260 — original record = handoff record `.claude/handoff/history/20260930-150000-사용량-슬롯-실사용-피드백-결정-구현-대기.md` · `…/20260930-153000-사용량-슬롯-실사용-피드백-결정-8건-구현-대기.md`)**
- **Minimum top/bottom size = lower the split-ratio limit so only a 30px minimum per cell (`MIN_PANE_PX`) remains (「아예 겹쳐서 올릴 수 없는 정도가 아니라면 괜찮다」)** — the cause (the 10% limit) was confirmed statically from the code (§3 #105). ADR-0260.
- **The stage is by width only — it does not change because of height** — if the height is short, the bottom is cut off and hidden (replaces §3 #99 · #106 ①).
- **「몇 분 전」 = once on the company header of the popup, with no clock icon, 「Claude 플랜: max · 1분 전 ⟳」 (「아이콘 2개 나와서 난잡 … 1분 전 하고 바로 리프래시 버튼이 있으면 리프레시라는게 인지가 되니깐」)** — the age that was written on every window row is dropped.
- **Popup ⟳ = one per company** — the single global ⟳ of the small display stays as is (reverses ADR-0258 decision 1 for the popup only — ADR-0259).
- **(User answer 2026-09-30 (this session — original = `.claude/handoff/attachments/usage-slot-user-answers-2026-09-30.md`) — confirmed before the document review)** the age on the header = the oldest age of the two windows, 5-hour and weekly · per-model windows are not included · amber is the same value (the user picked the main recommendation B after seeing the mockup — 「B이긴한데」 … 「ㅇㅇ 그렇게해」 · §6 #29 closed) · the ratio limit is released on both sides (`RATIO_MIN`·`RATIO_MAX`) · the reason the reset word was dropped = 「리셋이라고 하면 뭔가 지저분함」 · the reason the height fallback was dropped = 「아마 높이 때문에 가장 긴거에서 중간거로 안가고 짧은거로 바로해서였나 잘 기억이 안남」 (★the user's memory is uncertain★) · the reason a company that is turned off is also hidden from the popup = 「다른데가 그래서 그랬고, 보여주면 갱신도 해야되잖아. n/a로 표시해도 되긴 한데 어쨋든 표시가 애매하고 다른곳도 그래서」.
- **Reset notation = unified to the hourglass icon (lucide `Hourglass`) — small display and popup (「그냥 모레시계 통일로 하자」)** — tooltip and screen reader are 「리셋 11:29」. The user was told that there is no precedent.
- **On/off = keep as is** — popup 「슬롯에 표시」 · a company that is turned off also disappears from the popup (the same as the convention).
- **Amber = when a value is more than 30 minutes old, 「N분 전」 in amber** — automatic interval 15 minutes × 2 = `STALE_AFTER_SECS`.
- **Do not fetch anew when the popup opens (the Orca way)** — it is the convention of tools that have periodic queries.
- Two things that ended without a decision: the percentage direction was a mistake (remaining amount = PRD R1 as is) · 「1분 전」 = the time the value was received (explanation only).

**Confirmed (2026-10-01 — amendment 20 · real-use feedback 2 · §3 #107·#108 · ADR-0261 — original = `.claude/handoff/attachments/usage-slot-user-answers-2026-10-01.md`)**
- **The ⟳ button itself spins — remove the per-company 「갱신 중」 marker (「일단 그게 깔끔할것같긴한데」 → main recommendation (A) → 「ㅇㅇ 그렇게 하면 될듯?」)** — the global ⟳ of the small display = spins if even one of the target companies is being queried (manual or automatic) · popup per-company ⟳ = spins if that company is being queried · at least one full turn · when the query ends it finishes the turn it was on and stops (main proposal — accepted with the same answer) · it spins even under reduced motion and e-ink (the repository's rotation rule). Rejected = (B) the button also spins and the per-company marker is kept as well.
- **Pressing again during a query = only the rotation restarts from the beginning, with no new query (「근데 다시 한번 연속으로 누르면 처음부터 돌리면 될듯?」 → 「ㅇㅇ 알았어. 이해함. 그대로 하면 될것같고」)** — the discard window = from the moment of pressing until that query's result arrives · no fixed interval (ADR-0258 decision 3 as is). A press while it is finishing the turn after the query ended = a new query + rotation from the beginning (one branch). Rejected = also sending a new query on a re-press during a query (overturns ADR-0258 · 429 risk — §6 #21).
- **Popup alignment = (B) one table across both company cells (「ㅇㅇ 나로 해줘.」 — main recommendation)** — columns = name | bar | % (right end) | reset time (hourglass + time) | remaining time 「(… 뒤)」 · name column = the longest name in the whole popup · fixed-width digits · all row spacing equal · a today-reset row has a blank after the time (notified at presentation). Rejected = (A) reset time and remaining time as one block (aligned only to the hourglass start point — ★the user's original wording of the rejection reason was not recorded★).
- A question that is not a decision (for the record): 「둘중에 하나 실패하면 어떻게 되는거임?」 → main answer (code and test basis · not measured in the app) = independent per company — the failed company keeps its last value + 「!」 badge · the same sentence on the top line of the popup · amber when over 30 minutes. Partial refusal of the global ⟳ (some companies refused) is unspecified (an existing open item).

**Target:** none. Build it with the defaults of 「확인 필요」 of PRD D16–D21.

## 3. Internal choices I made (report)

1. Pickup transport = StatusSink hook + pump drain — the `open_spawn` port would change about ten call sites and make the decoder side-effecting.
2. The observation time is stamped by the daemon (the `mono` at the moment the service applies it) — the decoder does not hold a clock.
3. A window where only `resets_at` changed without `used_pct` has its % erased — the old % of a new window is a wrong value.
4. The 「새로 들어옴」 of D12 = only a window that carried `used_pct`.
5. Add `Upstream` (unclassifiable) and `NotInstalled` (the first state of R31) to `ProbeError`.
6. An unknown error of Claude A → `Upstream` (= query failure), and only wording that reads as a limit → `RateLimited{None}` — showing an unknown error as "거절됨" would deceive R31 and block ⟳. The automatic query time is the same either way (the max formula). 4-0: `rate_limits: null` of a success response is 「한도 정보 없음」 only when an explicit `rate_limits_available: false` is with it, and otherwise `Upstream` — the 2.1.280 schema writes null as 「받아 오지 못함」 and the CLI-side 429 also arrives as this null (§1-3). So on this version `Rejected` is unreachable (§6 #21).
7. Codex RPC error → `Unauthenticated` if it is auth wording, otherwise `Upstream`.
8. Cooldown and deadline constants are issued as `UsagePolicy` by each vendor's `usage.rs` — the daemon and `usage/` hold no vendor policy (「백엔드 확장」 · ADR-0004).
9. probe env = `CLAUDE*` removal + allowlist (`CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` · `CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN`) + the same four keys as the probe — stripping even the account and authentication could change the D13 default login. Codex inherits as is.
10. probe cwd and settings files = a fresh temp folder per query (deleted afterwards) — do not rely on the working folder, and do not put JSON through `cmd.exe` quoting.
11. ISO 8601 → epoch seconds = `chrono` (already in `Cargo.lock` 0.4.45) — **newly entered as a direct dependency of agent (a dependency-report item)**.
12. Time = integer epoch seconds (core `i64` · wire `u64`) · elapsed time is relative seconds.
13. Per-model windows = Claude `model_scoped[]` + `seven_day_opus`/`_sonnet`/`_oauth_apps` that hold a value, the weekly window of a Codex non-default bucket (D8).
14. Two entrances → the same service: webview = a dedicated wire request · LLM = the bus. The webview has neither a bus producer nor the `CommandReply` branch of `handleEvent` (`messages.rs:555-576`).
15. The wire ⟳ request (`RefreshUsageLimits`) = `Detached` — so that a waiting request does not block terminal input on the same connection (the subscription `UsageSubscribe` is the opposite, `InOrder` — #55).
16. `REPLY_WAIT_MAX = 5초` — below the bus deadline of 7 seconds (`command_delivery.rs:464` · the CLI silence limit of 10 seconds `types.rs:256`) and the shell reply cap of 30 seconds. If exceeded, cache + `in_flight`.
17. Bus handler = synchronous port + `blocking_handler`, and the daemon adapter waits on the blocking pool with the std channel `recv_timeout` — the daemon entrance drives only handlers that finish at the first poll (`control/commands.rs:104-125`), and `Handle::block_on(timeout)` risks a timer panic during shutdown → abort.
18. Bus declaration = the agent `commands.rs` block — the producer of the vocabulary (backend words · row type) is agent (ADR-0155 decision 1) and it uses the `AgentBackend` of `agent.new` as is. The real implementation is a daemon port.
19. All numbers are 「머지 시점 master 값 + 1」 — `PROTOCOL_VERSION` · agent catalog · shell catalog.
20. The missing show_* of `setSlotContent` = keep the existing value (read-modify-write inside the lock) — ~~the human path is whole replacement.~~ The human path also takes the same merge with a per-cell `set_usage_slot` (amendment 17 — §3 #102 ⑤).
21. (Amendment 15 — with the usage menu items gone, this slot has no consumer · §3 #102) Menu ☑ = an optional cell of `SlotMenuItem.checked` + slot content in ctx — since the title is fixed at build time (`slotMenu.ts:43-52`) there is nowhere to carry state, and if a contribution reads the store it breaks ADR-0064.
22. Width stage = comparison of hidden-render measurements — character counts and px constants break across locales (R3).
23. `STALE_AFTER_SECS = 1800` (frontend) — the age flows on the frontend.
24. danger color = the github family (`#f85149`/`#cf222e`) — the existing green and yellow are the github family (ADR-0062).
25. Vendor file separation (`backend/claude/usage.rs`·`backend/codex/usage.rs`) — reduces the `mod.rs` conflict surface.
26. Do not attach `help` to the frontend registry id `usageSlot.*` — it does not overlap with the bus `usage.refresh` (`viewCommandBridge.ts:40-58`).
27. OS branches (launch flag · tree kill · exit code) = the one module `usage/process.rs` — the two existing inline copies of `CREATE_NO_WINDOW` are a parallel-branch conflict surface, so they are not merged.
28. Clock seam `UsageClock` — decisions are always measured with this clock (amendment 2: even after the scheduler wakes — the clock of the wait timer is not trusted · #58).
29. Expiry latch = daemon-owned, evaluated at scheduler · request · pickup application · subscription add — if decided by the frontend wall clock, an old % revives on a rewind.
30. ~~Refusal storage = `usage_rejects.json` · `usage_service/reject_store.rs` · read first at assembly — values and cooldown are not stored (R29).~~ ★Removed by ADR-0284 — 2026-10-08 (§3 #109)★: the refusal deadline is not stored either — memory only.
31. Windows not installed = after EOF, `cmd.exe` exit code 9009 — no path-search crate is brought in.
32. Utilization normalization = 0–1 ×100 then rounded at the fourth decimal place — prevents 0.55×100 = 55.00000000000001 → floor 44 (true value 45).
33. State key = `(vendor, account_key)`, and `account_key` is always `"default"` — so that a future client attached to several daemons can tell the source apart. Codex `accountId` is the future source.
34. ~~Polling = frontend `USAGE_POLL_MS = 60_000` · visible = window visible ∧ slot intersecting ∧ connected · `pending` shared by the webview store~~ — ★replaced (user decision 2026-09-27 — amendment 2)★: subscription type (#54–#66). What remains = the visibility decision (amendment 6 — the shell decides by window hidden ∧ active tab #78 · it was once the webview's slot intersection) · `pending` is only the ⟳ wait.
35. ~~Accepting a pickup → screen delay ≤ 60 seconds~~ — ★replaced (user decision 2026-09-27)★: when a value changes, push it out at once (#47).
36. ~~Pickup = waiting map + merge at request time~~ — ★replaced (user decision 2026-09-27)★: apply straight from the pump thread with no waiting map (#45). The old devices (per-cell merging · the mono guard of merged items · the 「합친 결과 = 차례 적용 결과」 property test) are all removed.
37. `REJECT_MAX = 24시간` — the longest observed `Retry-After` is 1 hour (query §0-3), so normal values are not cut, and only absurd waits produced by a broken header or a clock shift across a restart are cut.
38. ~~`REFRESH_MIN_SPACING = 30초` (per company, shared by human and LLM) — so that an LLM loop does not mash ⟳ and burn the upstream limit. The cooldown (15 minutes) is still ignored (D11).~~ ★Deleted (amendment 15 — user decision 2026-09-29 · §3 #102)★.
39. Query = detached std thread + Job-close kill — the alternative `UsageService::shutdown` adds a step to the shutdown procedure (`lib.rs:763-765`) whose ordering risk is already written down, and cannot cover a crash. The Job covers both.
40. Non-default profile pickup block = `UsageGate` of `open_spawn` — so that another account's values are not mixed into the `"default"` cell.
41. Query deadline = one that covers write · read · exit wait all — so that a child that closes stdout and does not die cannot hold the completion guard. `KILL_WAIT = 2초` is the upper bound for waiting until the file lock is released after the kill.
42. Temp folder = exclusive creation with a random name under the data folder `usage-probe/` + sweep at startup — claude trusts and reads the `--mcp-config` file. The data folder belongs to one daemon, unlike the shared OS temp folder, so sweeping is safe.
43. Canonical key = `usage_probe_for(..)?.key()` — words with different case at each entrance gather into one cell.
44. No-panic parsing = drop per cell — so that, under release abort, a response with one changed shape does not kill the daemon.

**2026-09-27 amendment — internal choices attached to push on change immediately (user decision)**

45. Pickup = no waiting map — the decorator `UsageObserveSink` (renamed from `UsageInboxSink` so that the name does not suggest stacking) calls the service on the pump thread, and the service, inside the book lock (leaf · poison tolerant), stamps `mono` → window merge → D12 → latch (`wall`) → revision → snapshot, releases the lock, and emits. The `merge_window` caller is one — sequential application stands by structure. Cost = the pump thread takes one short in-memory leaf lock (no I/O or await · amendment 2: also the registry lock briefly at emit time after releasing the book — amendment 3: inside it only target collection + JSON once, sending outside the lock — amendment 4: inside it only target collection, JSON and sending outside the lock — §1-4 concurrency).
46. `RESET_SAME_TOLERANCE = 60초` — if two `resets_at` are within 60 seconds, the same reset.
