The reset time varies below one second from call to call (§6 #17), so a one-second wobble must not erase a correct %, and a truly new window differs by hours. ★Scope of application (amendment 2 — main decision): the % is erased only when the old and new `resets_at` are both Some and differ by more than 60 seconds★ — old None → new Some is learning the reset (% kept), and a new None ~~of pickup~~ of an observation (regardless of source — amendment 12 #88) is not carried (kept).
47. Emit = whenever the revision rises (amendment 2 — replaces the old 「줍기 변화·조회 완료 둘뿐 · 조회 시작·요청 때 래치는 발행 안 함 · 60초 폴이 나머지를 몬다」): a pickup change (merged to 1 second) · query start · query end · latch · subscription add (to that connection only). A pickup that changed nothing is not emitted.
48. ~~Emit port `UsagePublisher{publish(snapshot)}` — the all-connection fan-out implementation `UsageFanout`~~ — ★replaced (amendment 2)★: per-connection exit port `UsageOutlet{send(frame)}` (amendment 3 — `frame` = snapshot + the JSON that the encoder port `UsageEncoder` built once per emit · amendment 4: built outside the registry lock and only when there are targets, and `None` = skipping that emit) — the consumer owns the trait, and the connection layer gives the real `ConnUsageOutlet` at `on_connect` (#59). New wire event `UsageLimitsUpdated` · no new transport. `DaemonStatusSink` still encodes only what the core gave.
49. revision = monotonic `u64` per cell, +1 on any change, a cell of the wire snapshot — answer and broadcast carry the same single sheet. The frontend keeps only the per-company maximum and discards smaller ones (a shell hand-off taken before a broadcast may arrive after the broadcast — the webview does not use the value of the ⟳ answer, amendment 5 · the emit order of two pump threads may differ from the application order). A daemon restart is from 0 → the frontend forgets only when the socket marker (`socketEpoch`) grows (amendment 3 #75 · amendment 4: the second rule that forgot on leaving `connected` was removed — a late disconnect transition would let in a small revision of the same socket again).
50. The `served` of a broadcast = always `Cached` — `served` is the cell that speaks of the query a request waited for, and a broadcast has no request. Freshness is carried by the window's `age_secs`~~·`source`~~. (Amendment 7: the cell itself was removed from the wire snapshot — #81 · amendment 12: the window's `source` was also removed — #88)
51. Shell delivery = the usage arm at the `emit_broadcast` call site (`connection.rs:1015`) + one `DaemonEvents` method (amendment 2: `labels` argument — #63 · amendment 3: `socket_epoch` argument — #75 · amendment 4: the arm is at the call site, not inside `emit_broadcast` — that function is a synchronous function that receives only `(events, ev)`, so it has no interest state, socket marker or `sink`) — the same path as the existing broadcast. Since the `_ => {}` of `emit_broadcast` silently drops an unknown variant, a recording-fake test is the only wall.

**Choices attached to the 4-0 facts**

52. 「한도 정보 없음」 = not `ProbeError` but the observation's `limits_unavailable` — because it is not a failure (clear the values, stamp `last_query`, and the next automatic query is at the normal cooldown). Visible state `Unavailable` (the sixth) · ~~during `Unavailable`, when a pickup `used_pct` arrives, `Ready` · other abnormal states are not changed by pickup (R22)~~ ★replaced (amendment 12 — #88)★: an observation holding a window value (% or reset) goes to `Ready` from any abnormal state.
53. `Failed.next_attempt_in_secs` = `u64` — since `next_auto` is always defined (no reference point = `reject_until` or now), the `None` branch of `Option` has no meaning. Saturates at 0. The code switch is Stage 4.

**2026-09-27 amendment 2 — internal choices attached to the subscription type (user decision)**

54. Subscription = one declarative set per connection (`UsageSubscribe{vendors}` whole replacement · empty = unsubscribe · disconnect = empty) — paired messages (subscribe/unsubscribe) rejected: if one unsubscribe sheet is lost, the query keeps running. The last message is the state (the user accepted).
55. `UsageSubscribe` = `command_request_id` `None` (no answer — the shell has no waiting ticket and the connection task writes it directly, amendment 3 #74) · `dispatch_order` `InOrder` (consecutive replacements must be in arrival order for the last to win · no await in handling) · what the webview sent is blocked by `forward_daemon_command` (authority = the interest the shell computes from the layout — amendment 6 · the same place as the `Subscribe` block BLOCK-1).
56. Registry cell = created by `on_connect` and removed by `on_disconnect`, and replacement edits only existing cells — a late replacement that overlapped with disconnect cleanup cannot revive a cell (the command registry precedent). It is made after the two greetings (the same reason as `agent_conn.rs:206-215`).
57. First snapshot order = rely on revision — replay→live (sending while holding a lock) would have to break the book leaf lock, and a snapshot is one sheet that is the whole state, so 「가장 높은 것이 이긴다」 is enough. The gap is closed with 「등록 먼저·스냅숏 나중」 + 「적용 먼저·대상 열거 나중」 (§1-4).
58. Scheduler = detached std thread + std channel `recv_timeout` + `Weak` (amendment 3: wake = `sync_channel(1)` + `try_send`, `Full` ignored — the pump thread wakes it, so it must not block · sleep and deadline arithmetic is saturating/`checked` · the query thread also holds only `Weak` so a service drop is immediately the end of the thread) — the same reason as the query thread and bus wait (the runtime drop does not wait · no step in the shutdown procedure · no risk of a tokio timer poll during shutdown — #17·#39). The tokio task alternative would make two sets of fake time, the fake clock (`UsageClock`) and tokio's. The decision is split out as the pure `plan_tick` (`book.rs`) and measured with a fake clock. `SCHEDULE_MAX_SLEEP = 60초` — the wait timer's clock may not count sleep (§6 #2) and the reset time is wall clock, so lateness after resume or a clock shift is bounded to 60 seconds (the cost of waking once a minute is a few lines of decision).
59. Emit target = only connections that subscribed to that company (per connection `FrameSink::try_send` — amendment 3: inside the registry lock target collection + JSON once, sending after releasing the lock → amendment 4: inside the lock only target collection, JSON once and sending after releasing the lock · ADR-0006 · the same shape as `net/src/ws.rs:207-221`) — all-connection fan-out rejected: the meaning of subscription would vanish, and a different `subscribed` per connection (amendment 6 — #78) cannot be carried in one sheet. Cost = a saturated connection is cut (the same treatment as the agent output sink · unlike the list broadcast that skips on saturation — §6 #24).
60. `USAGE_PUBLISH_COALESCE = 1초` (main decision) · pickup only (amendment 12: not a source rule but a rate control for the value changes that crowd in through the observation entrance — #88) · final emit guaranteed (the scheduler emits the last snapshot at the deadline) — the stream emits events one after another per window (Claude one window at a time). Query start, end and latch are rare and 「갱신 중」 must not be late, so they are not merged.
61. Shell = interest authority (amendment 6 — not a per-window-label reported set; the shell computes it from its own layout #78) · when it grows, at once · when it shrinks, `USAGE_INTEREST_SHRINK_DELAY = 1.5초` (main decision — saves the two sheets of release and add at a popout · amendment 3: growing and shrinking are compared with `sent` #74) · sent back at every new socket. It differs from the eager resubscribe that ADR-0046 removed (idempotent whole replacement) · and it does not run into ADR-0164 (the amendment of ADR-0046 decision 2 — reason = a refusal storm against the empty registry of a restarted daemon) which rejected socket-triggered resubscription: `UsageSubscribe` has no existence premise and is not refused, so no storm occurs (amendment 3 — a stronger basis than idempotence) · it also differs from the 「창구 닫힘에 명령 담아 두기」 that ADR-0195 rejected (it rebuilds from state, not from commands) — the review judged it valid.
62. Shell snapshot cache = (connection, company) · cleared when the socket is lost · hand-off = the shell command `get_usage_snapshot` that the webview pulls after the broadcast hookup (amendment 6 — the old shape was the return value of the interest report #78) — if it waited for an emit, a new popout window would stay empty until the next broadcast (up to 15 minutes). Amendment 3: cell = highest revision + received time · relative cells corrected when handed over · bound to the socket marker (`socket_epoch`) (#75) — the reason it does not hold the last arrival is that the daemon's first snapshot goes out outside the registry lock, so a small revision may come after a large one (§1-4). Without the correction, an old cache would rewind the age by that much. ★Boundary with ADR-0046 「src-tauri = 무상태 라우터」 (amendment 4)★: the scope of its decisions 1 and 3 is **agent output** — decision 1 means the router that removes the output mirror (`AgentBufferStore`·`OutputViewStore`·per-view cursor) and passes a frame through to the window Channel reading only its header, and decision 3 is 「출력 진도(seq/cursor/버퍼) 금지 — 허용 상태는 요청 부기(epoch·single-flight)뿐」. It is not a rule that the shell is stateless in general (layout, windows, tabs and slots are shell-owned — `layout/manager.rs`). This cache is outside that: it holds one sheet of whole state per company with no progress (the highest revision wins — there is no cursor or seq to match), and clears it when the socket is lost. Still, it is true that it resembles the class that ADR removed (「데몬이 가진 것을 한 벌 더 쥐고 맞추기」), so the cache is used only in one place, the hand-off. ★Rejected — hand-off by a daemon read instead of the shell cache (main decision · amendment 5 — doc-aware re-review)★: the shape in which, when a new window asks for values, the shell sends a read-only wire request and delivers its answer only to that window. ① The generic request/answer path does not carry `socket_epoch` (`forward_daemon_command` does not know the variant — #75), so a read hand-off needs a dedicated request path owned by the connection task — a new wire request + waiting routing inside the connection task, which is exactly where the overlapping `request_id` defect occurred (regression net `src-tauri/tests/daemon_client_pending.rs`). The cache hand-off is one shell command, so the daemon round trip is 0 (amendment 6) ② The cache rules (highest revision · received time · relative-cell correction · `socket_epoch` binding) are pure functions inside one shell module (`usage_interest.rs`), so `lib_unit` measures them. ★It is true that the lesson of ADR-0046 resembles this cache★ — the 「미러 유지 + 동기화 정교화」 (「동기화 버그의 답은 동기화 제거지 정교화가 아님」 — that ADR 「거부한 대안」) that ADR rejected is exactly this shape of adding rules to the cache. ★Condition for reconsideration = if the cache rules grow beyond the current four, move to a daemon read hand-off★.
63. Shell broadcast = emit to all windows + filter by the payload's `labels` (`window:tabs-updated` precedent) — `labels` = windows that have a usage slot (in any tab · amendment 6 — the old criterion 「관심을 보고한 창」 disappeared with the report #78). A window without a usage slot has no reason to receive. It does not rely on the target decision of Tauri `emit_to` (it is unconfirmed whether the webview's global `listen` receives regardless of the target — following the precedent is cheaper).
64. ~~Two layers of self-healing (webview re-report · shell resend · shrink healing)~~ — ★replaced by #78 (amendment 6)★: the webview side vanished with the report, and the two shell rules became a single reconciliation of the broadcast's `subscribed` (§1-7 「대조」).
65. ~~Do not put 「연결됨」 into the webview's interest~~ — ★replaced by #78 (amendment 6)★: the webview does not compute interest, so the question itself does not exist.
66. ~~The webview reports an empty interest once as soon as it comes up (clearing the old interest after a refresh)~~ — ★replaced by #78 (amendment 6)★: a refresh does not touch interest (§1-9 #12).
67. Remove the wire `GetUsageLimits` — since #62 chose the cache hand-off, nobody would call it (webview = subscription + ⟳ · LLM = bus · shell = cache hand-off) — if #62 is reversed it is revived (amendment 5). One each of variant, correlation pair and golden goes away.

**2026-09-27 amendment 2 — internal choices attached to failure tracing (user decision)**

68. Wire `UsageStateDetail{kind: String, code: Option<i64>, upstream: Option<String>}` on every abnormal-state variant — not mixed (classification · number · text). `kind` is a string (so that a new classification does not break an old shell's deserialization — the frontend shows it as is without translation).
69. Raw text = only in the UI and LLM rows, logging forbidden (logs may be shared — main decision) · base `mask_secrets` + 200 characters · `Debug` covers it (both agent `UpstreamText` and wire `UsageStateDetail` hand-implemented) — it deliberately changes the `ProbeError` head rule (`usage/probe.rs:24-27`): string cells = our sentences for the log · raw text = a dedicated cell.
70. `ProbeFailure{error, detail}` wrapper + `From<ProbeError>` — the spawner seam and the `?` inside the querier stay as they are. The alternative of adding a cell to every `ProbeError` variant touches all nine variants.
71. Observation `limits_unavailable: bool → Option<UsageDetail>` — 「없음」 and its basis are one cell, so the two cannot diverge.
72. The raw-text cell holds only the values of the named cells (Claude `error`·two cells · Codex `error.message`) — the response line and stderr as a whole are not carried (the `accountId` of a Codex response).

**2026-09-27 amendment 3 — internal choices attached to review FIX (shell · frontend)**

73. ~~Interest report order = per-webview `seq` + shell-issued load generation + reissue of a report that is not of the current generation (amendments 3–5)~~ — ★replaced by #78 (amendment 6)★: there is no report path, so there is no order guard either. The alternatives rejected there (a webview random load marker · raising the generation on a page-load event · sharing an in-progress report · `seq` only) also lost their question along with the path.
74. `UsageSubscribe` transmission = one serial path (the connection task) · the set is taken by reading the interest at send time · `sent` = the set last sent on this socket (`on_socket_lost` → ∅ · new socket → send interest directly + `sent := 관심` · the growing/shrinking decision compares with `sent` · amendment 6: the resend decision is a single reconciliation of the broadcast's `subscribed` — #78). The side that changes interest puts in only a valueless nudge. Rejected: each caller reads and does `send_fire_and_forget` (if two changes cross between the read and the send, the old set is last — codex blind) · carrying the set in the nudge (it goes stale the moment it is carried).
75. The shell socket marker `socket_epoch` on the usage payload going to the webview (the ADR-0195 marker — a socket number that is never rewound · this name everywhere in this path, a bare `epoch` forbidden — amendment 4) — the frontend forgets the revision when it grows and discards when it is smaller · the shell cache is bound to this marker · the ⟳ answer (it has no marker because it is a generic forwarding path) does not use a value and resolves only `pending` — the value comes by broadcast (amendment 5 — replaces the old 「낼 때의 표식과 다르면 값을 버린다」 · §1-8). Amendment 4: this is the only forgetting rule — 「`connected` 를 떠날 때 잊기」 (the second rule of amendment 3) was removed. Rejected: connection-state transition only (it cannot see generation succession Connected→Connected — `daemon_client/mod.rs:395-398`·`connection.rs:702-709`) · ★a connection-state transition as a second rule alongside (amendment 4 — codex re-review)★: the disconnect transition comes by another path and may reach late after the new socket's payload, and erasing the floor then would accept a late-arriving small revision of the same socket — 「잊기만 하니 해가 없다」 was wrong · carrying a daemon identifier on the wire (§1-6 — the shell already tells sockets apart) · having the shell insert a marker on the ⟳ answer path (`forward_daemon_command` does not know the variant — `messages.rs:559-563`).
76. Tray hide = the shell removes interest (interest = computed from the active tab of windows with no hidden marker — amendment 6 #78 · `hide_main_ui`/`show_main_ui` call `set_visible` per window — one path of ADR-0229) — the webview does not decide window hiding. Rejected: webview `visibilitychange` (zero precedent in `src/` — the one that hides is the shell, so the shell already knows). Minimizing counts as a visible window and is not tracked (user decision 2026-09-27 — §2 · ADR-0229 decision 1 · §6 #28 closed).
77. ~~Interest report surface (the generation request and report of `agentClient` → `Transport` method)~~ — ★replaced by #78 (amendment 6)★: the remaining shell command is the cache pull alone (`agentClient.getUsageSnapshot()` → `Transport` method `requestReplay` form — the same rule: the store does not call `invoke` directly, ADR-0011 · transport seam ADR-0036).

**2026-09-27 amendment 6 — internal choices attached to the shell computing interest from the layout (user decision)**

78. **Interest = the shell computes it from its own layout (user decision 2026-09-27 — 「셸이 데이터만 들고있고 웹뷰는 그것기반으로 그려주는거잖아」 · §1-7 「관심」)** — the sum of companies turned on by usage slots in the active tab of visible windows (not hidden to the tray · minimized = visible). The pure functions (`interest_from_layout`·`usage_slot_windows`) are called inside the ViewManager lock at every place where the layout changes, daemon-side divergence is caught by a single reconciliation of the broadcast's `subscribed`, and ⟳ is passed on after a forced resend (§2). Basis = that data is already the shell's (windows · tabs · active tab · slot tree · slot content — ADR-0035 · the usage toggle is also slot content — `set_slot_content`). Rejected: ★the webview computes interest, reports it to the shell, and keeps the order as a rule (seq · load_gen · StaleGen reissue · shrink healing — amendments 3–5)★ — the report was a second copy of what the shell already had (the same class as the 「미러 유지 + 동기화 정교화」 that ADR-0046 rejected — 「동기화 버그의 답은 동기화 제거지 정교화가 아님」), and at every re-review a new ordering hole came out of that path (a late report from the page before a refresh · a late-arriving generation request · reissue stopped · ⟳ no response after a growth loss). After reaching the review fix limit, the root cause was confirmed with the user. Replaced records = #64·#65·#66·#73·#77 (only a one-line record is left) · #34·#61·#62·#63·#74·#76 have only the relevant phrases fixed.
79. `subscribed` = the daemon takes a copy of that connection cell's current set inside the registry lock and carries it (§1-4 「구독 명부」) — since the event differs per connection, JSON is built once per emit **per distinct set** (still once for a single shell — a generalization of the old 「발행당 한 번」). Rejected: building per connection (encoding as many times as there are targets) · appending a cell to the JSON the exit built (building the wire as a string outside the encoder).
80. A pending shrink ends **at the deadline** (`deferral_elapsed` — the timer lowers the wait first and puts in the nudge) — replaces the old deadline record + 2-second margin healing. Even if the nudge disappears, the wait has already ended and the reconciliation of the next snapshot fixes it by comparing against the interest. Cost = a snapshot that left before the one shrink sheet right after the deadline may make the same interest be sent one more time (idempotent · no feedback — §1-7 「대조」). No margin constant is set.
81. ⟳ answer = the existing `Ack{request_id}` — the dedicated answer `UsageLimits{request_id, snapshot}` was removed because nobody reads it (the webview has not read the value since amendment 5 · the shell passes it through the generic path · the bus does not use this wire). One each of variant, correlation pair and golden goes away. ★Then the `served` of the wire snapshot is always `Cached`, so it is removed from the wire in amendment 7 (§1-6)★ — amendment 6 had 「칸은 남긴다」, but that cell carried nothing and the frontend does not read it. `UsageLimitSnapshot` is a type newly brought in by this unmerged branch (Stage 1 `a2ba60e`) and goes out with the same `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump, so removing it now costs nothing (if removed after the merge, one more wire change would be needed). `served` remains in the daemon-internal snapshot answer and the bus row `UsageVendorRow.served` — the bus has requests, so `Fresh`/`Cached` have meaning. ★Amendment 8: the type `UsageServed` is also moved from protocol to the daemon `usage_service`, and the removal of the cell and type is pulled forward from Stage 5 to Stage 4 (§1-6 · §5)★ — the bus row is in the agent crate and agent does not depend on protocol (`crates/engram-dashboard-agent/Cargo.toml:17-18`), so it uses its own macro enum `Fresh|Cached`. If left in protocol, an internal type read only by the daemon decision would sit in the wire crate carrying an unused TS export. Before Stage 5 nobody reads the wire, so removing it in Stage 4 stands. Rejected: leaving only the type in protocol (amendment 7 — a wire type with only the daemon as its reader).
82. Webview pull triggers = once right after the broadcast hookup + usage slot mount — `initEventBus` runs at boot after the daemon ensure (`App.tsx:43-44`), so a slot may mount before the hookup. Slot mount alone does not stand 「구독 먼저」.
83. Recompute places = the real `SubscriptionSync` (`RouterSubs::resync`) + popup destruction + tray — the re-sync port of layout writes already does the same job (output routing) 「바뀐 레이아웃에서 데몬 구독을 다시 맞춘다」, and every write that receives it calls it (`apply.rs` · the LLM table also uses the same adapter). Rejected: one more port on the apply service (every write signature would grow) · computing at the layout notification (`LayoutEvents` — outside the lock) (the calculation order of two writes would cross).
84. Reconciliation resend = **one sheet per diverged snapshot**, no suppression · a `debug` log per sheet (amendment 8 · §1-7 「대조」) — a same-set replacement issues no first snapshot, and a latch emit is only once per window, so the feedback is finite (the latch evaluation before the registry lock may emit one sheet with the pre-replacement `subscribed` — one more same-set resend), the cost is bounded by event frequency (at most one small frame per event), and even a difference that cannot be filled is capped at one sheet per event and shows in the log. Rejected: ★the same-pair suppression guard (amendment 7 — the same (`subscribed`, wanted set) pair is not sent again until one side changes) — it blocked a needed resend as well (both reviewers)★ — an old record left by an earlier toggle round trip, and a correction resend the daemon refused with a saturated receiving line, both carried the same pair again and the divergence did not heal · time-interval limit — unnecessary because event frequency already bounds the cost.
85. **Amendment 9 (4b1 implementation — main decision 2026-09-28):** A. the time evaluation `eval_time` hardens the refusal end into a state (`Rejected` → `Failed` · revision +1 · emit target) — folding in the snapshot is only preliminary, and the scheduler also wakes at `reject_until` (4b2) · the 「래치 평가」 of the TRD = `eval_time` (including the refusal end) · called before every snapshot is taken · B. an active success (including no limit information) erases `reject_until` — erasing is also `reject_changed`: the completion guard stores with `reject_changed` (not 「거절이면」 — ~~storage~~ removed by ADR-0284, 2026-10-08) · C. `resets_at ≤ 0` = not carried (at the entrance — so that a value the wire cannot carry does not enter the cell and become an invisible change) · ~~D. source guard — if `Active` arrives at the pickup entrance, warn and ignore · if `Passive` arrives at the query end, warn and apply as active (`in_flight`·`last_query` are released as they are)~~ ★D replaced (amendment 12 — #88)★: merge and state do not look at the source — there is no guard that tells apart by source · E. per-model windows are received from one observation only up to `MODEL_SCOPED_MAX = 16` (from the front — if exceeded, the producer side has diverged, so warn — the two current producers carry only up to the cap) · an item beyond the cap is not evidence for `Unavailable`→`Ready` · the % of a per-model window is also evidence (amendment 12: evidence = % or reset · not only `Unavailable` but `Ready` from any abnormal state — that an item beyond the cap is not evidence stays as is, #88) · F. pickup `used_pct` Some + no reset + the held reset has already passed → drop the held reset (a new window — R32 「새 값이 올 때까지」) · H. a window whose % was erased by a changed reset also has its D12 newly-entered marker erased · ~~a pickup with no % during `Unavailable` = no change (wire 「값을 싣지 않는다」)~~ ★replaced (amendment 12 — #88): an observation carrying only a reset is also made `Ready`★.
86. **Amendment 10 (4b2 implementation — main decision 2026-09-28):** when the revision rises, that cell owes all subscribers one emit (a pickup value change = a debt that can be merged · anything else = at once) — the debt is paid only by an emit that goes to all subscribers (`broadcast_sheet` · `Coalesce::PublishNow` · `TickPlan.publish`) and the first sheet of a subscription does not pay it · even if the service misses an emit, the next tick pays it (if it is a subscribed cell · at the latest within `SCHEDULE_MAX_SLEEP`) · a latch or refusal end that stands inside `apply_passive` is a debt to be paid at once · paying and taking a snapshot are one call inside the book (all three return the snapshot with it) — if the lock is retaken to pay, a debt that stood in between would be erased · the scheduler does not touch the debt of a cell outside the subscription (the union copy may be stale) · the sleep of `TickPlan` is always greater than 0 (default = `SCHEDULE_MAX_SLEEP`) · request decisions follow the row order of the table as is (even if one is in progress, the earlier row comes first) · `plan_tick` receives the subscription set as a collection (`&BTreeSet` as is — the `Ord` of `UsageVendorKey` was added in 4c) · a vendor that cannot be changed over the wire is not registered · `TickPlan.publish` = (key, snapshot).
87. **Amendment 11 (4c implementation — main decision 2026-09-28):** add `revoke` to the exit port — release (`on_disconnect`) and an overwriting duplicate `attach` call it outside the registry lock, after which `send` is a no-op and the exit does not hold the sink (the `Target` it holds becomes quiet) · real = `Mutex<Option<Arc<dyn FrameSink>>>` — `send` takes only a copy inside the lock and `try_send` is outside the lock (ADR-0006 as is — do not send while holding) · what remains after release is only one copy held per in-progress `send`, dropped after that `try_send` once · `attach` receives the exit by value and wraps it with `Arc` inside — the same object cannot enter twice, so an overwritten exit is always reclaimed (`Arc`·`Box` batch implementation · no `Clone` exit that holds cells in separate parts — the real one holds the cell directly) · `Target.subscribed` is a `Arc` set (no copy) · `deliver` receives the target by value and drops it immediately on sending (no keeping).
88. **Amendment 12 (user decision 2026-09-28 — 「유저에게는 조회 줍기 구분하지말고 그냥 주고 데몬도 거기에 맞춰서 구성해」 · §2):** a value is a value — the book does not look at the source of an observation. ① **One merge:** for any observation, the windows and cells carried (`used_pct`·`resets_at`) overwrite, and those not carried (None · missing) keep the held value · the per-model window list and plan are replaced whole when carried, otherwise kept — a query success follows this rule too (withdrawal of the asymmetric 「조회 null·빠짐 = "없음"으로 비움」 of PRD D18 · the two rows of the §1-4 event table). A latch is released only for a window that carried a new % (releasing the latch of a window that was not carried would revive the old %). ★Accepted consequence (review 2026-09-28)★: if an account never carries some window again, that window stays holding its last value — after the reset passes, the latch stands and it shows as 「리셋됨 — 갱신 대기」, and stays so until a daemon restart or a new value. The per-model list is different — if the producer read that container and there are 0 items, it carries an empty list (a carried empty list = whole replacement) → a per-model window that disappeared is erased. ② **The window-passed rule stays · source-independent:** if a % with no reset arrives and the held reset has already passed, drop the held reset (#85 F) · if only a reset is carried and it differs from the held reset by more than `RESET_SAME_TOLERANCE`, erase the old % (#3·#46) · also erase that window's D12 marker (#85 H). ③ **State:** an observation that carries even one window value (% or reset) goes to `Ready` from any state (`Unavailable`·`Failed`·`NeedsLogin`·`NotInstalled`·`Rejected`) (detail erased) — replacing 「다른 비정상 상태는 줍기가 안 바꾼다」 of #52 and 「`Unavailable` 중 % 없는 줍기 = 무변화」 of #85. The query answer `limits_unavailable` is still `Unavailable` · a query failure is a failure state + values kept (R22 — a failure does not carry values) · a query success is always `Ready` even with no window value at all (the account answer the query gave — what pickup cannot give). ④ **Refusal:** even when it becomes `Ready`, `reject_until` remains inside — no automatic or ⟳ query before the deadline (R24) · only the visible 「거절됨」 disappears. The refusal end (`eval_time` — #85 A) hardens only the `Rejected` cell into `Failed` and does not turn the `Ready` cell back into `Failed` (the refusal end folds only the `Rejected` state, so the `Ready` cell stays — no new code, coder report). ⑤ **D12 source-independent:** if the two windows after the reference point each received a new % (by either pickup or query), the cooldown reference point is initialized (the reference-point cell = `fresh_reset` — the old name `passive_reset` was changed in the code). ⑥ **wire:** the window's `source`·`UsageSourceKind` were removed from protocol (`domain.rs` · re-export `lib.rs` · `bindings/UsageSourceKind.ts` deleted — coder report) · the observation's `source` (§1-1) is kept — producers and tests (about 12 files) carry and read it, but the merge and state rules do not read it · removal is a separate later item · there is no source guard (#85 D) · the `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump is as is in Stage 5. ⑦ **The 1-second merge (#60) stays** — it is not a source rule but a rate control for the value changes that crowd in through the observation entrance (`observe`). A state change that a value made at that entrance (→ `Ready`) is also a debt that can be merged — if a latch or refusal-end fold stands in the same call, emit at once (#86). ⑧ **Order:** no time comparison — arrival order under the book lock, the later write wins (§6 #17) · a difference under 1 second is not told apart by design. Rejected (user): distinguishing the source so that only the query sets the state — the user does not need to know the source, and a new value coming means it is being updated. ~~★Remaining questions — what this decision did not address (main check)★: ⓐ the 「창 값 전부 비움」 of the query answer 「한도 정보 없음」 (§1-4 event table · PRD R31) has a different grain from ① so it is left as is ⓑ the answer when ⟳ is pressed within the refusal deadline while `Ready` (the `Rejected{retry_in_secs}` row of the §1-4 decision table) and how the popup ⟳ disabled condition (§1-8 — 「거절 중」) matches the visible state.~~ ★Closed (main decision 2026-09-28)★: ⓐ the clearing of window values for the 「한도 정보 없음」 answer stays as is — it is an answer in which the account stated 「한도 없음」, not a source rule (§1-4 event table · PRD R31) · only plan follows the unified rule (replaced if carried · kept if not carried) ~~ⓑ ⟳ within the refusal deadline while `Ready` = the existing behavior as is — no query · the cache answer carries `Rejected{retry_in_secs}` (§1-4 decision table) · how the UI shows it (including the popup ⟳ disabled condition — §1-8) is Stage 7.~~ ~~★ⓑ open — awaiting user decision (2026-09-28)★: the closing above was wrong (review) — during a hidden refusal (a value has come and the cell is `Ready` · `reject_until` remains) the code cannot emit `Rejected{retry_in_secs}`: the snapshot moves `Ready` to `Ready` and the decision `Judgment::Rejected` does not carry the period, and the wire carries nothing about a hidden refusal — ⟳ quietly ends with no query. Options: A = the state stays `Ready` + one cell in the snapshot and answer for the remaining refusal wait (seconds) → 「N분 뒤 가능」 in the ⟳ place (main recommendation) · B = during the refusal deadline the state keeps `Rejected` even if a value arrives (numbers updated) — an exception to ③ 「값이 오면 `Ready`」 · C = as is (⟳ quietly ignored) + correct the wording of PRD D11 and §1-8. PRD D11 (「거절 중에는 막고 "거절됨 — N분 뒤" 를 보인다」) and the §1-8 popup ⟳ presuppose the visible `Rejected` state — leave them as is until a decision.~~ ★ⓑ closed — C (user decision 2026-09-29 — 「n분뒤 가능은 구현 짜증나잖아」 · the main session chose C instead of B with the user's agreement)★: a visible refusal (a refusal in which no value has come) = as before, state 「거절됨 — N분 뒤」 + ⟳ disabled · a refusal while a value has arrived and shows as `Ready` = ⟳ quietly answers from cache (no query goes out · no indication). Basis = in that state values are newly coming in (that is why `Ready`) and ⟳ has nothing to add · ⟳ within the refusal deadline issues a query in no case (R24 — even a 10-minute interval got 429 · 1-hour `Retry-After` observed). Rejected: A (a remaining-refusal-wait cell in the snapshot and answer + UI) — implementation cost (user) · B (keep the refusal visible even if a value arrives) — adds an exception to 「값이 오면 `Ready`」 and a special case in the refusal-end fold (main · the user agreed). The code is already C — what changed is wording (PRD D11·D22 · §1-4 decision table · §1-8 popup ⟳).
89. **Amendment 13 (4d2a implementation — main decision 2026-09-29):** A. the query-thread start seam `ProbeThreads` (real `OsProbeThreads` = a detached std thread `usage-probe`) — placed to test the thread-start `Err` · B. what the book lock wraps = `Desk{book, runs}` — `runs` = the number of finished queries per key `finished` · the last success number `last_ok` · blocking joiners, and async joiners wait on one tokio `watch<u64>` (the finished count) per key · the target a joiner waits for = `finished + 1` at registration — so a query opened by the scheduler has nothing to note separately · C. `served = Fresh ⟺ last_ok ≥ target` — `Fresh` only when the query waited for (or a later query) succeeded · D. the answer at the wait deadline = cache + `in_flight = true` · `Cached` · E. a hidden refusal (#88 ⓑ C) is answered by the refusal branch of the decision table with a cache snapshot — while hidden, that snapshot's state is `Ready` · F. the completion guard is made **before** launching the thread — if the launch is `Err`, it ends on the caller's thread with `Err(Spawn)`, and a querier panic ends with the guard's `Err(Io)` during unwind (release is `panic = "abort"`, so that is the test build's share) · G. a key with no querier ends at once with `Unsupported` without a query · ~~H. refusal storage = `save_lock` {book {all-cell refusal snapshot} → `store.save`} — file I/O is inside `save_lock` · outside the book lock (§1-4 「경로별 락 차례」 as is).~~ (H was removed by ADR-0284 — 2026-10-08 · #109) ★Accepted consequence (4d2a review)★: the sender of a bus joiner that gave up at the deadline stays in `runs.blocking` until the query ends — the query's deadline contract (deadline + `KILL_WAIT` + folder-delete retries) bounds that length · a querier that breaks the deadline contract is not bounded by the service side (`in_flight` remains) — accepted as the contract of the §1-4 event table 「조회 끝」 row · the completion guard is kept outside the launch closure until the launch becomes `Ok` (tightening — 4d2a review applied). The inline 「4d2a·#89」 marks (§1-4 head · constants · concurrency · query driving · assembly · §4 request · join · lifetime) point to this — the two lines `request`·`request_blocking` in the §1 skeleton are also this amendment.

**2026-09-29 amendment 14 (Stage 8 document cleanup — Stage 5–7 implementation follow-ups and review FIXes applied, main decision)**

90. **The hidden marker is erased only on window destruction** — only `UsageInterest::forget_window` (window-destruction only) erases the label from `hidden`, and an ordinary `recompute` (every other place where layout or visibility changes) does not (§1-7 「창 소멸」). Because, if the hidden marker were erased in the gap of phase B of moving a slot to another window (outside the lock — building the new window's webview), a hidden popout could be counted as visible during that gap.
91. **If a pending shrink is recomputed with the same interest, the generation and deadline are kept** — if `shrink_pending` already stands in `recompute` and the interest is the same as the previous recompute, a new generation is not drawn (§1-7 「셸 상태기」 · `usage_interest.rs:190-193`). An unrelated (interest-unchanging) recompute is kept from pushing back the 1.5-second shrink timer that is already running — 6a review FIX.
92. **The link permission file = a dedicated `capabilities/usage-links.json`** — the permission that narrows `opener:allow-open-url` to two URLs (the Claude and Codex usage settings pages) is not placed in `default.json`·`popup.json` but in one separate file shared by the windows (`main`·`slot-popup-*`) (§1-7 「링크 권한」) — keeping the list in one place prevents drift between two files.
93. **A ⟳ socket write failure does not break the connection task loop · a superseded socket sends only the refresh** — even if writing the subscription frame fails, `send_request` only wakes that refresh's `reply` with an error on the spot and does not leave the loop, and if the socket is really dead, the **next** `select` sees the disconnect by itself (§1-7 「⟳」). On a socket where the interest state already looks at a newer socket (superseded), `sync(.., true)` gives `None`, so the subscription frame is omitted and only the refresh frame goes out like an ordinary command — the subscription of that socket is sent when the new socket opens.
94. **Correct the 「반쪽만 나가는 경우 없음」 of the §4 shell test row** — if the subscription write fails, the refresh does not go out at all so both are absent, and **if the refresh write fails after the subscription succeeded, the subscription has already gone out alone on the socket** (§4 「셸」 row). The actual invariant is "a half request does not remain stuck in pending", and the subscription going out alone is a resendable idempotent state update, so it is safe.
95. **Fix the margin (about 2 seconds) of `REPLY_WAIT_MAX = 5초` against the 7-second bus command deadline with a const assert** — `assert!(REPLY_WAIT_MAX + 1초 < CommandDeliveries::DEFAULT_DEADLINE)` (`usage_service/bus.rs:22-27`) keeps it at compile time. The difference that the wait cap is measured from the moment it enters the handler while the bus deadline is measured from the moment the slot opens (blocking-pool delay) is absorbed by that margin, and that if the pool lags more than that, TIMEOUT goes out instead of the answer is a known and left remainder (5b review FIX).
96. **The TS type of `detail_code` diverges between two surfaces — awaiting user decision.** The macro accepts the `i64` cell as is (§6 #27 closed) so `UsageVendorRow.detail_code: Option<i64>` stood, but the `bindings/UsageVendorRow.ts` that ts-rs emitted is `detail_code?: bigint | null`, whereas the wire `UsageStateDetail.code` that carries the same value is fixed by hand as `number | null` (`protocol::domain.rs`). In the 5b review codex gave FIX (type mismatch) and Claude gave PASS, so they split (CLAUDE.md 「불일치는 메인 임의 판정 금지 → 사용자에게」) — whether to fix it, and if so which side to align to (narrowing the bus row to `number` or widening the wire to `bigint`), is for the user to confirm in the final document.
97. **Frontend reconnection pull (a deviation from the TRD, main decision) — `resyncAfterReconnect` also calls `usageStore.pull()`.** This TRD originally set no pull on the reconnection trigger (on the premise that the loss during a disconnect is filled by the new socket's first snapshot — §1-9 #4). To cover together the remainder codex pointed out in the 7a review (if the first pull right after startup exhausts its retries and fails, there is no trigger afterwards) and the doc-aware review finding (a case where, as in generation succession, the socket marker does not change and that window misses broadcasts while disconnected), a third trigger ⓒ (reconnection *re*-transition — excluding the first connection) was added. Even if the same snapshot is received again, the revision comparison of `merge` filters it out, so it is idempotent.
98. **New menu `enabled(ctx)` — the `Command.when` extension was undone (main decision, awaiting user confirmation).** The first implementation (`b59a7ac`·`664d011`) widened the existing `Command.when` to be argument-based in order to flow usage state into the menu, but after the two-party review (codex · Claude) FIX, the extension was undone (`626279c`) and a new additive cell `SlotMenuItem.enabled?: (ctx) => boolean` was created, riding on the two-step invariant of ADR-0064 (only command + registerSlotMenu registration) — ~~the assembly site (LayoutLeaf) subscribes and carries a generic field such as `usageRefreshable` in ctx~~ at that time the assembly site (LayoutLeaf) subscribed and carried the generic field `usageRefreshable` in ctx (ADR-0252). The undoing itself is a main orchestrator decision and, as of the 2026-09-29 session, is before user confirmation. ★`910c214` (amendment 15 — #102) removed all usage menu items and with them removed `usageRefreshable` and LayoutLeaf's usage-store subscription★ — the `enabled`·`checked` cells and the 「조립처가 ctx 로 싣는다」 rule remain, and there is no state carried now.
99. ~~**`UsageSlot` width stage = looks at height as well as width (stage 2 → stage 3 fallback, R31).** If the hidden-render height of stage 2 exceeds the slot root height, it falls to stage 3 even if the width fits (`pickStage` — `components/slot/UsageSlot.tsx`). The `!` badge of R31 appears at every width stage, but if stage 2 is cut off by a line wrap, the badge may vanish from the cut line — so it goes down instead to stage 3, which never wraps, and keeps the badge always visible (7b review FIX).~~ ★Replaced (amendment 19 — user decision 2026-09-30 · #106 ① · ADR-0259 decision 6)★: the stage is chosen by width only — if the height is short, the bottom line is cut off and hidden, and the badge on that line is hidden with it (an accepted cost).
100. **Correct §1-9 scenario #9 (popout) — phase A does not emit `Defer`.** `move_slot_to_window` phase A (inside the lock) only copies the source content into a temporary View, and that temporary View crosses phase B (outside the lock — building the new window's webview) while being in no window's tabs. The source slot itself stays alive and its content stays visible until phase C (inside the lock — insert the temporary View + close the source slot) (`apply.rs:691-699`) — so interest never becomes a proper subset of `sent` across phases A to C, and the frames going to the daemon are **0** (6c review FIX).
101. **Document maintenance recommendation — from now on, anchor by symbol name instead of line number.** In this Stage 8 cleanup, several old line pointers in §1-7, §1-9 and §4 had already been pushed off by the refactors in between (old-name moves · function splits) — pointing by symbols that do not shift when code moves (`fn`·`struct` · constant names) reduces that drift. Line numbers remain only as an aid for checking nearby context (not enforced — a recommendation).
102. **Amendment 15 — ⟳ placement · on/off location · 30-second interval · reset notation (user decision 2026-09-29 · ADR-0258).** ① Manual ⟳ = one global button on the small display (right of the grid · at all three width stages · `refreshUsageLimits` per company turned on · hidden if both companies are off) — ~~the per-company ⟳ in the popup and~~ the menu ⟳ are removed (§1-8 「팝업」·「메뉴」 · ★the popup's per-company ⟳ was revived by amendment 19 — #106 ② · ADR-0259★). The disable-on-refusal and quiet-cache-answer (§1-4 decision table · #88 ⓑ C) are applied to that button as is. The source record of the old 「세 곳」 (no user original) = ADR-0258 「맥락」. Peers = `docs/research/usage-manual-refresh-peers-2026-09-29.md` (a global refresh is common · blocking a re-press during a query exists everywhere confirmed from source · a time cooldown is rare). ② On/off = the bottom row of the popup 「슬롯에 표시: ☑ Claude ☑ Codex」 · all usage items (⟳·☑) of the slot right-click menu are removed — the commands `usageSlot.refresh`·`toggleClaude`·`toggleCodex` remain as the LLM path. Popup details only for companies turned on (a company turned off is not subscribed so it has no value) · if both are off, the small-display guidance = 「클릭해서 표시할 항목 고르기」 · only the on/off row in the popup. The popup is no longer view-only (PRD R7). So `checked`·`enabled(ctx)` (ADR-0252) of #21·#98 lose their consumer in this slot — ★open★: by main decision (2026-09-29), this change leaves the shared foundation (the `SlotMenuItem` cell) untouched · whether to remove it when there is no consumer is deferred. ②-2 implementation behavior (recorded at the request of code review): the small-display ⟳ ignores the press as a whole if even one of the target companies has a query in progress (user decision 2026-09-29 — 「조회가 도는동안 받기전까지 다시 누르면 무시」 · at worst until the query deadline, Claude 15 seconds · Codex 30 seconds · ★amendment 20 — that press still issues no new query, but the ⟳ rotation restarts from the beginning (#107 ②)★) · the daemon separately joins per company · pressing ⟳ with the popup open leaves the popup open (the ⟳ row counts as inside the popup — it means that it is counted on the popup side in the outside-click decision, and ~~the popup itself has no ⟳~~ the popup's own per-company ⟳ is amendment 19 — #106 ②) · Esc on ⟳ = close the popup, focus is not moved. ★Partial refusal — implementation behavior made explicit (main 2026-09-30 · the user may look at it again)★: the targets = among the companies turned on, those that are not a visible refusal (`Rejected` state — the `blocksRefresh` of `usageStore.ts`) (a company that has not yet received even one value is included — ⟳ is the path that calls the first value) — if only some are refused, only the rest are refreshed · if the targets are 0 (all companies turned on are a visible refusal), ⟳ is `aria-disabled` (not `disabled` — it holds focus and only blocks the press) and the press does not reach the command · 「진행 중」 = that company's ⟳ answer wait (`pending`) or the snapshot `in_flight` · the `usageSlot.refresh` command re-picks the targets from the store at press time, and if after drawing a refusal arrives before the press so that the targets are 0, it does not swallow quietly but returns with an error (the button path leaves only a log). A refusal while a value has arrived and shows as `Ready` is included in the targets, and the daemon answers from cache with no query (#88 ⓑ C). Tests = `UsageSlot.test.tsx` 「작은 표시의 ⟳」 · `usageCommands.test.ts`. ③ Remove `REFRESH_MIN_SPACING` (#38) and the 「간격」 row of the decision table — an in-progress query is joined (no new query until the result arrives) · no query during refusal (R24 · D22) stays. Accepted risk = Claude 429 from mashing (it can last about an hour — the design doc of the ClaudeBar repository `docs/providers/claude/design.md:61-62`, query survey §0-3 · vendor unconfirmed · that observation is a direct call to the B address and sharing under A is 「가능성 높음」 — query survey §0-4) · ★the only brake on the Claude side is the in-progress lock★ — CLI 2.1.280 returns a 429 as `rate_limits: null` and it shows as `Failed` and does not reach `Rejected`, so the block during refusal is not applied (§6 #21) · values keep coming in through conversation in the meantime. ④ Small-display reset time = a word instead of the ↻ glyph — 「리셋 00:58」 · 「resets 00:58」 (★the word was changed by amendment 19 to an hourglass icon — the date rule below stays · #106 ③ · ADR-0259★) · if the reset is on a day that is not today, date → time rather than weekday — 「리셋 10/1 01:48」 · 「resets Oct 1 01:48」. The popup's absolute reset time follows the same rule (date + time with no weekday). Peers = `docs/research/usage-reset-time-format-peers-2026-09-29.md` (dates are the most common and both vendor tools use dates · date+weekday does not exist). The rejected notations and their reasons (the trade-offs at presentation) = ADR-0258 「거부한 대안」. ★A place that departs from PRD R28 (dates and times are `Intl`)★ — the date part is assembled by `t('usage.resetDateTime', {month, day, time})` of `usageFormat.ts`'s `formatResetAt` inserting the numeric month and day (only the time `Intl`). So an English 「resets Oct 1 01:48」 cannot be issued yet — a locale formatter is needed when the en table is added (there is no en table yet, only `ko.ts` — ADR-0258 decision 4 implementation note). ⑤ **Amendment 17 —** the on/off write (the per-cell IPC design = main decision 2026-09-30 — an implementation detail of ADR-0258 decision 2 · code review round 2. ★That the IPC accepts an exception that conflicts with 「슬롯 콘텐츠 변경은 `set_slot_content` 단일 경로」 of ADR-0063 and its rejected alternative 「variant별 전용 command」 was a user decision 2026-09-30 — option A · B (widening `set_slot_content` to partial cells) rejected · §2★): newly add the webview Tauri command `set_usage_slot(view_id, slot_id, show_claude?, show_codex?)` (`src-tauri/src/commands/layout.rs`) to open the shell's existing in-lock per-cell merge `apply::set_usage_slot` (before, it was reachable only through the bus `layout.setSlotContent`). `usageSlot.toggleClaude`·`toggleCodex` send only their own cell as an absolute `!current[field]` value by `viewStore.setUsageSlot` — it is not a `set_slot_content` whole replacement. Reason = a whole write built from the content the webview drew last would let quick consecutive clicks on other checkboxes (or a concurrent LLM write) overwrite each other. Rejected = a client-side wait-selection workaround (if the echo order is reversed or partly fails, there is still a race — rejected in review). ★Deferred follow-up (not done)★: the in-window `layout.setSlotContent` path (`__engramCmd`/cdp) still fills the missing usage display cells from the webview copy (the `mergeUsageShows` of `src/commands/tabCommands.ts`) — switching it to `set_usage_slot` would make it atomic. ★Pressing the same cell again before it is reflected (echo) = keep as is (user decision 2026-09-30)★ — meaning: the checkbox draws the slot content the shell gave as is and does no optimistic update (ADR-0035). So a re-click before reflection sends the same absolute value (`!current[field]`) computed from the still-old value one more time, and the slot content does not change (the second click is effectively ignored). ★Do not 「고치지」 ("fix") it with an optimistic toggle or a flip inside the shell★ — the app-wide handling is decided in the follow-up topic T-36 (`docs/tracking.md` · status and precedents = `docs/research/pre-echo-reinput-2026-09-30.md`). **Implementation (구현:): `910c214` (amendments 15 · 17 — amendment 16 is the `6598291` of #103).**
103. **Amendment 16 — record of the small display overhaul (user decision 2026-09-29 · implementation `6598291`).** ① Instead of the company name (including the abbreviation at stage 3), a small **company icon** (`components/slot/VendorIcon.tsx`) — the current shape is a placeholder and the final shape is undecided · the small display carries the company name on the icon as the accessible name and `title`, and the popup has the icon (decorative) + name text · the `!` badge (R31) is right next to the icon at every stage (outside the ellipsis). ② Stage 1 = put the two company rows on **one grid** and align the columns (icon | window name · bar · % · reset × 2) vertically (numbers tabular-nums · % right-aligned · narrow gap between bar and %) · stage 2 = in one grid the icon spans that company's two window rows · stage 3 = icon + number. The width and height stage measurement (§1-8 · #99) is measured with a hidden render of the same new shape — no px threshold. ③ **Remove the ⚠ next to under-20% on both the small display and the popup** (amendment of PRD R10·R11 — user: it looks like an error mark and the number already says it) · the cue outside color is borne by the e-ink red bar (black fill + thick border). ④ The small display shows the **reset time** instead of the remaining time and puts the remaining time only in the popup (stages 1·2 — §1-8). The notation implemented at that time (「↻ HH:MM」 · weekly 「↻ 요일 HH:MM」) was replaced the same day by #102 ④ — 「리셋 HH:MM」 · on a day that is not today, date + time. The usage row color points only at base tokens (`--status-danger` · `--vendor-claude` · `--vendor-codex`) (no color literal — for later color presets' contrast). It is a small UI detail, so no separate ADR is set.
104. **Amendment 18 — ⟳ slot implementation (2026-09-30).** The user (2026-09-30): 「음? 리프레시 버튼이 왜이렇게 떨어져있어?」 → 「알아서 잘 붙이도록하고 자러간다 진행 ㄱ」. **① Placement = user decision** — ⟳ attaches right next to the content at every stage (the same direction as ADR-0258 decision 1 「격자 오른쪽」) · the stage 3 row shrinks to the content (`fit-content` · `maxWidth: 100%`). **②–⑤ Method and cost = main decision (under the delegation of 「알아서」 above)** — ② the width used for stage measurement = slot width − ⟳ slot width − margin (it no longer measures the width of the content cell). ③ Stage 3 puts an invisible headroom as wide as the 「갱신 중」 marker at the end so that ⟳ does not move during a query — when resting, the gap between the content and ⟳ widens by about 14px more on top of the summary button's right margin (6px). ④ The hidden measurement copy of stages 1·2 always counts the 「갱신 중」 marker in — so that a query start or end cannot flip the stage. ⑤ Accepted cost = stages 1·2 step back to the next stage about 14px early · when a value changes (digit count · `!` badge) ⟳ still moves · narrow stage 3 (when the slot is narrower than the stage 3 content): when resting the headroom drops out by line wrap so there is no gap · during a query the number is cut about 14px early (the same as HEAD) · ⟳ does not move · side effect = at stage 3, pressing the empty band to the right of ⟳ does not open the popup (the row shrinks to the content so that band is outside the summary button). **Implementation: `d88dfee`.** ★Amendment 20 removes ③ and ④ — there is no 「갱신 중」 marker to keep · the cost of about 14px in ⑤ also disappears with it · ① and ② stay as they are (#107 ⑥ · ADR-0261)★.

**2026-09-30 amendment 19 — eight real-use feedback decisions (user decision · §2 · implementation: `603248d` — the gate is §5 9)**

105. **Amendment 19 (a) — lower the split-ratio limit (user decision 2026-09-30 · ADR-0260).** The user: 「아예 겹쳐서 올릴 수 없는 정도가 아니라면 괜찮다」 → leave only a minimum of 30px per cell (`MIN_PANE_PX`).
    - ① **Cause decision = hypothesis confirmed (static · code) — no on-screen measurement.**
      - The shell `set_split_ratio` first clamps the requested value with `clamp_ratio` (`[RATIO_MIN, RATIO_MAX]` = `[0.1, 0.9]` — `src-tauri/src/layout/tree.rs:16-17`·`:23-25`).
      - If the owning window's canvas and metrics are known, it clamps once more — `lo = RATIO_MIN.max(edge)` · `hi = RATIO_MAX.min(1 − edge)`. It is `edge = min_pane_px / L` and L = canvas axis px × split box axis width (`manager.rs:460-476`). So **if L > 300px, the 10% limit (> 30px) wins** — the floor of the upper cell of a 900px-high split is 90px.
      - On-screen dragging also puts the snapshot limit into the same formula (`ratioRange` — `src/components/layout/splitPreview.ts:100-113`) (`viewStore.ts:296` → `WindowLayout.tsx:292` → `useSplitDrag.ts:186-206`). The pointer value is also clamped to that range (`ratioFromPointer` `splitPreview.ts:119`). So it stops at the same place.
      - Checked whether there are other walls — the cell frame and the usage slot have no CSS min-height (only the canvas's `minHeight: 0` in `src/components/layout`·`src/components/slot`). The usage slot root is `height:100%` + `overflow:hidden` so it does not push the cell out (`UsageSlot.tsx:239-247`). The height condition of `pickStage` (`UsageSlot.tsx:234`) changes only the display stage, not the cell size (#106 ①). The split guard `SplitTooDeep` applies only to new splits.
      - ★Remaining uncertainty★ — L of that split on the user's screen was not measured. If L ≤ 300px, the wall was 30px and not 10%, and then this change does not resolve it.
    - ② **What changes — `RATIO_MIN` to a small positive number, and `RATIO_MAX` to 1 − that value (main decision (internal constant) — `0.01`/`0.99` · subject to review).**
      - ⓐ It is not removed to 0. The extreme values the geometry `boundary` (`geometry.rs:143-152`) planted directly in the tree are clamped by this limit to prevent a zero-area leaf. `spatial.rs:456` `degenerate_ratio_produces_no_zero_area_leaf` (ADR-0068 §0 FIX-4) relies on that guard.
      - ⓑ If `0.01`, at L ≤ 3000px 30px wins. Only in a split where one axis exceeds 3000 CSS px is the ratio limit slightly larger than 30px.
      - ⓒ When canvas and metrics are unknown (before a report · right after a popout), only the ratio limit applies (the `if let Some` of `manager.rs:461`). Then the LLM `split.setRatio` can make a 1% cell (10px at 1000px). The zero-area filter (`manager.rs:478-489`) stays as is.
      - ⓓ Both sides — the wall of the lower (right) cell is `RATIO_MAX`. Both are released so that any cell can shrink to 30px (user decision — user answer 2026-09-30 · ADR-0260 decision 1).
    - ③ **Code places — block (a).**
      - Shell constant = `src-tauri/src/layout/tree.rs:16-17`.
      - Shell tests — `tree.rs` `ratio_clamps_to_the_shell_bounds` (`:628-638` — the literal values 0.05·0.1·0.9) · `manager.rs` `set_split_ratio_clamps_to_the_ratio_bounds` (`:2278`) · `px_minimum_keeps_both_sides_at_least_min_pane_px_on_the_split_axis` (`:2344`). The 「바깥: L = 1000 → m/L = 0.1 이라 비율 한계가 이긴다」 (`:2367-2370`) of this test flips to px wins at the new limit.
      - The assertion (`:2414-2419`) of `without_canvas_or_metrics_only_the_ratio_bounds_apply` (`:2400`) that a `0.01` request is clamped to `RATIO_MIN` is re-picked to fit the new value. `an_ancestor_ratio_that_would_collapse_a_deep_leaf_is_too_small` (`:2524` — the root 0.9·0.1 chain) is re-measured at the new limit.
      - Tests written with the constant name still stand — `geometry.rs:573-585` · `spatial.rs:456` (as long as the limit is positive).
      - ★The command summary the LLM reads★ = the 「셸이 0.1~0.9 로 자르고」 (`src-tauri/src/layout/commands.rs:232`) of the `split.setRatio` declaration. The guard test `both_ratio_commands_define_the_ratio_as_the_a_side_share` (`src-tauri/tests/layout_commands.rs:1126-1144`) measures whether `format!("{}~{}", RATIO_MIN, RATIO_MAX)` is in the summary — with the new value it must be written as 「0.01~0.99」 to stand. ~~The test file does not need to be touched.~~ ★Wrong (implementation — review verdict)★: `set_ratio_writes_through_the_apply_service_and_clamps_out_of_range_values` in the same file had the old limit hard-coded as literals (the expected value 0.9 · 0.1 for an out-of-range request · 0.05 for a below-floor request) — the expected values were changed to `tree::RATIO_MIN`·`tree::RATIO_MAX` and the below-floor request to 0.005.
      - Comment = `src-tauri/src/layout/types.rs:87` (「0.1~0.9」). Attach the `// ADR-0260` anchor of the new value to the `tree.rs` constant (next to the existing `// ADR-0227`·`// ADR-0140`).
      - Frontend code holds no constant (snapshot `ratio_min/max`). `splitPreview.ts` is not touched because its formula is as is. The test fixed values (`src/components/layout/testing/rects.ts:21` `TEST_RATIO_BOUNDS` · `useSplitDrag.test.tsx:144` · `Splitter.test.tsx:51`) are pure tests that take the limit as an argument, so they stand without adjustment — whether to adjust is the coder's call.
    - ④ **Tests and verification.**
      - Shell = `cargo test -p engram-dashboard --test lib_unit` + `cargo test -p engram-dashboard --test layout_commands` (command summary guard). New assertions: at L = 1000 · m = 30, a `0.01` request → `0.03` (px wins) · the lower cell of an upper/lower split also down to 30px (the `RATIO_MAX` side) · no metrics → up to the new `RATIO_MIN` · extreme 0.0 → no zero-area leaf (FIX-4 as is).
      - Frontend = `npm test` + `npx tsc --noEmit`.
      - On-screen measurement (`/qa full`) — drag the divider of an upper/lower split all the way and see whether the upper and lower cells each stop at about 30px. Also see whether the slot breaks when a usage slot cell is shrunk to near 30px (together with #106 ①).
    - ⑤ **Order (stands wherever it stops)** — the constant, its shell test and the `split.setRatio` summary (`commands.rs:232`) are one commit (if only the constant is changed, the `tree.rs`·`manager.rs` test and the summary guard of `layout_commands` go red). Cleaning up the frontend fixed values is a later commit, and it stands without it.
    - ⑥ **Old description** — `docs/process/S21-flat-split-renderer/trd.md` writes 「0.1~0.9」 as a decision and constant (§2c · §3 D1·D2 · constant list). ADR-0260 amends it. Whether to fix the body of that document is out of scope this time (main judgment).
106. **Amendment 19 (b) — usage slot real-use overhaul (user decision 2026-09-30 · ADR-0259).** ①–⑥ divide the seven decisions of ADR-0259 into six strands (decisions 1 and 2 of ADR-0259 are bundled into ② · the 「결정 N」 below are all ADR-0259 numbers). ⑦ is the blocks and order.
    - ① **Stage = by width only (ADR-0259 decision 6 · replaces #99).**
      - Remove `fitsHeight` (`:234`) from `pickStage` (`src/components/slot/UsageSlot.tsx:225-237`). If stage 1 does not fit, only check whether stage 2 fits by width.
      - Then `rootH`·`s2h` (`:206`·`:214`) of `Measures` and the observer's two `put` (`:330`·`:335`) have no reader — remove them together. `SUMMARY_PAD_Y_PX` stays as the summary button margin (`:393`). Fix the comment (`:225-228`) too.
      - If the height is short, the root's `overflow: hidden` (`:243`) cuts off the bottom — no new code.
      - Accepted cost — the `!` badge on a cut line is also hidden (PRD R31 amendment · ADR-0259 「영향」).
      - Tests — flip `UsageSlot.test.tsx` 「2단이 슬롯보다 키가 크면 3단」 (`:1182`) and 「높이 되돌림」 (`:1571`). If the width allows stage 2, it is stage 2 even if the slot is low, and the slot root cuts it off. 「관찰 대상 = 슬롯 루트 · ⟳ 자리 · 두 측정 사본」 (`:1547`) stands as is (the root keeps measuring because of width).
    - ② **Popup company header = 「<아이콘> <이름> 플랜: max · 1분 전 ⟳」 (ADR-0259 decisions 1·2).**
      - Add one age and a per-company ⟳ to the `VendorDetail` header (`UsageSlot.tsx:1051-1067`). The per-row age of `PopupWindowRow` (`:1115-1119` · comment `:1108`) is removed. Fix the header comment (`:1031-1034` — 「회사별 ⟳ 는 두지 않는다」) too.
      - There is no clock icon. If there is no plan, the age comes right after the name (the separator is in the wording template — R28). ~~The place of the 「갱신 중」 text (`:1062-1066`) is left as is (main proposal).~~ ★Amendment 20 — that text is removed · that company's ⟳ spins (#107 ①)★.
      - ⓐ **The header age = the oldest age of the two windows, 5-hour and weekly — per-model windows are not included · ④ amber is the same value (user decision — the user picked the main recommendation B after seeing the mockup: 「B이긴한데」 … 「ㅇㅇ 그렇게해」 · user answer 2026-09-30 (this session — original = `.claude/handoff/attachments/usage-slot-user-answers-2026-09-30.md`) · §6 #29 closed).**
        - The age comes per window (`UsageWindow.age_secs` · `usageFormat.ts:91`). If none of the 5-hour and weekly windows has a value, no age is written (even if a per-model window has a value).
        - The case where they differ (code fact) — the ages of the 5-hour and weekly windows differ after an observation that updated only one window (the same when a window is missing from a query response — PRD R16 · the book changes only the windows carried) — for pickup, it is the old claude `rate_limit_event` shape (only one top-level `rateLimitType`) or a case where one window of `unifiedWindows` is missing (`crates/engram-dashboard-agent/src/backend/claude/usage.rs:5-8`·`:53-76`). ★Per-model windows are a different case★ — pickup does not carry per-model windows (`backend/claude/usage.rs:90` · `backend/codex/usage.rs:81` — `model_scoped: None`) and only the query fills them (`backend/claude/usage_probe.rs:359`). The book changes the per-model list only when it is carried (`crates/engram-dashboard-daemon/src/usage_service/book.rs:587-589`). On top of that, a pickup that carried both windows stands `fresh_reset` (`book.rs:591-595`) and pushes the next automatic query later (`next_auto` — `book.rs:554-558`). So while a conversation continues, the 5-hour and weekly are always fresh but the per-model windows may age by hours.
        - Cost ① = even if pickup has just updated only one window, the header shows the age of the less fresh window (the side that does not hide staleness). Cost ② = an aged per-model row does not show its staleness on screen — ⟳ receives that row anew too.
      - ⓑ **Per-company ⟳ press rule (main proposal — the small-display ⟳ rule applied to that one company).**
        - If that company is a visible refusal (`blocksRefresh` — `src/store/usageStore.ts:135-137`), it is `aria-disabled` (focus remains — the same reason as `RefreshButton` `UsageSlot.tsx:462-465`).
        - If that company is updating (`pending` or `in_flight`), the press is discarded (★amendment 20 — there is still no new query, and only the rotation restarts from the beginning · #107 ② · ADR-0261 amends ADR-0259 decision 2 for the on-screen response only★). A refusal while a value has arrived and shows as `Ready` is answered by the daemon from cache (#88 ⓑ C as is).
        - The press is inside the popup, so the popup stays open and focus stays on ⟳.
        - Accessible name = 「<회사> 사용량 새로고침」 (a new wording key — `src/i18n/ko.ts` `usage:`).
      - ⓒ **Command path (main proposal — CLAUDE.md 「LLM-우선 제어」: ride on the existing surface).**
        - Add an optional argument `vendor` to `usageSlot.refresh` (`src/commands/usageCommands.ts:35-50`). If present it is that one company, and if absent it is all companies turned on as now. If that company is turned off or `blocksRefresh`, return an error — do not swallow it quietly like 「대상 0」 does now.
        - There is no new global handle. The other way = a new id per company (symmetric with the toggle `usageSlot.toggleClaude`·`toggleCodex` but the surface grows with the number of companies) — the main session chooses.
      - Tests — flip `UsageSlot.test.tsx` 「팝업엔 회사별 ⟳ 가 없다」 (`:692`). Change 「창별 절대 리셋 시각 · 값마다 나이 …」 (`:639`) to once on the header. Change 「30분 넘은 값 → … 팝업은 나이를 보이고 흐리지 않는다」 (`:496`) to amber (④). New tests = pressing the per-company ⟳ → only that company is refreshed · refusal → `aria-disabled` · updating → press ignored · the popup stays open. Add the `vendor` argument (present · absent · company turned off · during refusal) to `usageCommands.test.ts`.
    - ③ **Reset = hourglass (ADR-0259 decision 3).**
      - There are two places — the small display (the reset cell `UsageSlot.tsx:723-730` of `WindowCells` — currently `formatResetClock` = 「리셋 {time}」) and the popup (`PopupWindowRow` `:1110-1114` — currently `resetsAtIn` = 「리셋 {time} ({duration} 뒤)」).
      - Both are lucide `Hourglass` (`lucide-react` ^1.23.0 — `package.json:29` · `hourglass.mjs` really exists in the installed copy) + the visible text = `formatResetAt` (`usageFormat.ts:150`). If today, the time, otherwise date + time (the date rule of ADR-0258 decision 4 as is). The popup attaches the remaining-time parentheses as they are (R2).
      - Tooltip (`title`) · screen reader name = 「리셋 11:29」 — the current `usage.resetClock` wording (`ko.ts:160`) is left as the name template and `formatResetClock` (`usageFormat.ts:158`) builds that name. Whether to put the remaining time in the popup's name is a main proposal: it is already in the visible text, so the name is only 「리셋 11:29」. The icon itself is `aria-hidden` and the name is borne by the wrapping element. ★Main decision (implementation — review verdict)★: the wrapping element (`ResetMark`) carries only `role="img"` + `title` — the screen reader name stands from `title`. If `aria-label` were attached together with the same text, the `title` not used for the name would attach as the description and the same words would be read twice (accname). The tooltip (`title`) is a user decision, so it is kept.
      - ~~The wording key `resetsAtIn` is changed to a template without the word (e.g. 「{time} ({duration} 뒤)」).~~ ★Main decision (implementation — review verdict)★: the popup's reset place = `<ResetMark/>` (hourglass + time · `role="img"` · `title` = 「리셋 11:29」) + a space character + the sibling `span` (`data-usage-reset-in`), and the wording key is `resetIn: '({duration} 뒤)'` instead of `resetsAtIn`. The remaining time was moved out as a sibling outside the marker because of an accessibility rule — the name is only 「리셋 HH:MM」 and `role="img"` hides the inner text from assistive technology, so if the remaining time were inside it would not be read. ★So the word order time → remaining time is in the code, not the wording template — it departs from PRD R28 (word order and joining marks are in the wording template) (an accepted cost)★. The space character between the two is for those who read DOM text (R27 — LLM · cdp), and the visible gap is drawn by flex `gap` (a text of only whitespace inside a flex is not drawn).
      - The reset cell of the stage-1 grid becomes narrower by the width of the word, so the stage threshold moves — there is no px threshold (R3), so there is no new code.
      - R27 (values and times are DOM text) stands on the time text. The word 「리셋」 drops out of the DOM text and remains only in `title` and the accessible name (main judgment — accepted).
      - Tests — `UsageSlot.test.tsx` 「리셋 시각은 1·2단에만(「리셋 HH:MM」 …)」 (`:376`) · 「다른 날의 리셋 시각은 「M/D HH:MM」」 (`:667`) · the `formatResetClock` assertion of `usageFormat.test.ts` is changed to the new shape. New tests = the icon exists · `title` = 「리셋 HH:MM」 · there is no arrow icon in the reset place (`RefreshCw` only on ⟳ and the updating marker — ★amendment 20: only on ⟳ · the marker is removed (#107)★).
    - ④ **Amber (ADR-0259 decision 4).**
      - If the age on the popup header is `isStale` (`usageFormat.ts:76-78` — `> STALE_AFTER_SECS` 1800), it is amber. `title` = 「30분 넘게 새로 들어오지 않은 값」 (= currently `usage.stale` `ko.ts:172`). The decision input is the age that ② ⓐ chose.
      - Color token (main proposal) — put a new `--usage-stale` in the three theme blocks (`src/styles/theme.css:9-11`) and point it at the base token `--status-blocked` (dark `#d29922` · light `#9a6700`). Conform to the no-color-literal rule (#103 · test `UsageSlot.test.tsx:1387`).
      - ★e-ink★ — `--status-blocked` is `var(--text)` (`theme.css:3`). There is no color cue and it only becomes darker than the dim text (`--text-muted`) (§6 #30).
      - The small display is as is (dims only the number — `ValueText` `UsageSlot.tsx:825-833`).
      - Tests — the 30-minute boundary (1800 seconds = usual color · 1801 seconds = amber — the same constant as the boundary of `usageFormat.test.ts:94-95`) · when it crosses over by the minute tick it changes without a request.
    - ⑤ **No query when the popup opens (ADR-0259 decision 5) — no code change, a record of the current behavior.**
      - The summary button click is only the `setOpen` toggle (`UsageSlot.tsx:386`). The popup (`UsagePopup` `:856-978`) does not call a query. The slot mount pull (`:182-185`) is the shell cache (#82 — no daemon round trip).
      - Basis = the convention of tools with periodic queries (Orca — survey 「결정」 section). Risk context = §6 #21 (the Claude 429 comes as `Failed` so the block during refusal does not apply).
      - Test (new regression net) — opening and closing the popup makes `refreshUsageLimits` calls 0 · the pull is only once at mount.
    - ⑥ **On/off kept as is (ADR-0259 decision 7) — no code change.** A company that is turned off is not in `views` (`UsageSlot.tsx:195-196`). So it is nowhere in the small display or the popup details, and remains only in the display toggle row (`ShowToggles` `:985-1029`) as a place to turn it on again. The test already exists (`UsageSlot.test.tsx:250`).
    - ⑦ **Implementation blocks — two, and the files do not overlap.**
      - (a) = #105 — `src-tauri/src/layout/` (`tree.rs`·`manager.rs`·`types.rs` comment · the `split.setRatio` summary of `commands.rs` + test) · summary guard `src-tauri/tests/layout_commands.rs` (~~just run it — nothing to fix~~ ★there is something to fix — the ratio-clamp test in the same file had the old limit hard-coded (#105 ③)★) · frontend test fixed values (optional). ★`splitPreview.ts` has no code change★ — the formula is as is and the limit comes through the snapshot.
      - (b) = this item — `src/components/slot/UsageSlot.tsx` · `usageFormat.ts` · `src/i18n/ko.ts`. ★Files needed beyond the first list★ = `src/commands/usageCommands.ts` (② ⓒ) · `src/styles/theme.css` (④ — if following the main proposal) · tests `UsageSlot.test.tsx` · `usageFormat.test.ts` · `src/commands/usageCommands.test.ts`.
      - There is no file that overlaps between (a) and (b) — they may go in parallel.
      - Order inside (b) (stands wherever it stops) — ① remove the height fallback (alone) → ③ hourglass (wording template + two places) → ② header age + remove row age → ② ⓑ·ⓒ per-company ⟳ + command argument → ④ amber (on top of the age of ②).
      - `// ADR-0259` anchor places (CLAUDE.md 「설계 결정 기록」 — one load-bearing line each) — `pickStage` (① — no height fallback) · the summary button `onClick` and `UsagePopup` (⑤ — no query on open) · the `VendorDetail` header (② — age rule: the oldest of 5-hour and weekly) · the two reset cells (③ — hourglass · arrow icon forbidden). On the (a) side, `// ADR-0260` on the `tree.rs` constant (#105 ③).
      - `prompts/engram-help.md` is not touched — `usageSlot.*` does not go on the bus because it has no `help` (#26 · `usageCommands.ts:5-7`).

**2026-10-01 amendment 20 — two real-use feedback decisions (user decision · §2 · implementation: `512850d` — the gate is §5 10)**

107. **Amendment 20 (a) — the ⟳ button itself spins · the per-company 「갱신 중」 marker is removed (user decision 2026-10-01 · ADR-0261).** For pointers the symbol name is canonical and the line is an aid based on `603248d` (#101).
    - ① **What is removed.**
      - Small display — `RefreshingMark` (the spinning arrow inside the icon bundle `NameUnit` · `data-usage-refreshing="<회사>"` · name 「갱신 중」) · the stage-3 empty space `RefreshingMarkSpace` · the branch in which the measurement copy always counted the marker (`view.refreshing || measure` of `NameUnit`).
      - Popup — the 「갱신 중」 text at the end of the company header (`data-usage-refreshing-text` of `VendorDetail`). The outside-press comment of the popup (「상세의 「갱신 중」을 보며 누르게」 — `onDown` of `UsagePopup`) is also adjusted.
      - What is kept — the wording keys `usage.refreshing`·`usage.a11yStatusRefreshing` (the summary button's accessible name `summaryLabel` keeps using them · ④).
    - ② **Rotation and press (user decision).**
      - The global ⟳ (`RefreshButton`) spins if even one of the targets (among the companies turned on, those that are not a visible refusal — the targets of the current `busy`) is being queried. The popup per-company ⟳ spins if that company is being queried.
      - 「조회 중」 = the current decision as is: `pending` (⟳ answer wait) ∨ snapshot `in_flight` (`refreshing` of `buildVendorView`) — automatic queries opened by the scheduler are included too (§1-4).
      - At least one full turn · when the query ends it finishes the turn it was on and stops (it does not stop abruptly).
      - A press during a query = does not call `onRefresh` (no new query, and no forced subscription resend before it — ADR-0258 decision 3 · ADR-0254 as is) · only the rotation restarts from the beginning. A press while it is finishing the turn after the query ended = an ordinary press (`onRefresh` + rotation from the beginning). If it is a visible refusal (`blocked` — `aria-disabled`), it does nothing as now.
      - It spins even under reduced motion and e-ink — the repository's rotation rule from the comment above `RefreshingMark` is moved over to the ⟳ side.
    - ③ **Main decision (implementation) — rotation time.**
      - Minimum rotation = one turn of the current `animate-spin` (Tailwind v4 `--animate-spin: spin 1s linear infinite` — 1 second). No new time constant is set. It is within the proposed range shown to the user (about 0.6–1 second).
      - It stops at a turn boundary (e.g. `animationiteration`) · restarting re-applies the animation from 0. The method is the coder's share and the conditions are three — at least one full turn · stop at the boundary · from the beginning on a re-press.
      - The rotation is `transform`, so it does not change the ⟳ slot or width — so even when a query starts or ends, the content and the ⟳ slot stay as they are.
    - ④ **Main decision (implementation) — machine-readable progress state (CLAUDE.md 「LLM-우선 제어」 · PRD R27).** Even after removing the visible marker, the per-company 「조회 중」 is kept in the DOM.
      - The global ⟳ (`data-usage-refresh`) = `data-usage-refreshing="<조회 중인 회사 낱말들>"` — separated by spaces (e.g. `"claude codex"` · if one, `"codex"`). If no company is being queried, the attribute is absent.
      - The popup per-company ⟳ (`data-usage-vendor-refresh="<회사>"`) = `data-usage-refreshing="<회사>"` only while that company is being queried · otherwise the attribute is absent.
      - `aria-busy` stays on both buttons (as now).
      - ★The attribute and `aria-busy` follow the query and do not follow the rotation★ — while it finishes the turn after the query ended, it is already gone. This matches the press at that time being an ordinary press (②). Whether it is rotating is not marked separately — tests and cdp look at the animation of the ⟳ icon (class · `getAnimations()`).
      - How to find = `[data-usage-refreshing~="codex"]` (whitespace-separated word match — catches both buttons and does not rely on word order). The attribute name is inherited from the marker being removed, but the element it attaches to changes to the ⟳ button — tests and cdp queries that looked for the old marker place (inside `NameUnit`) are fixed.
      - The per-company 「갱신 중」 (`summaryLabel`) of the summary button's accessible name stays as is — the PRD did not change it.
    - ⑤ **Main decision (implementation) — the stage measurement input changes.** The share in which the measurement copy (`data-usage-measure="1"`·`"2"`) always counted the marker disappears.
