So the inputs `s1`·`s2` of `pickStage` shrink by the marker width (about 14px), and stages 1·2 step back to the next stage that much later — there is no px threshold, so there is no new code (R3). The stage-3 empty space is also removed, so between the content of a resting stage-3 row and ⟳ only the summary button's right margin (6px) remains. `contentAvail` (slot width − ⟳ slot − margin) is as is — the ⟳ slot width does not change even when it spins.
    - ⑥ **Relation to 「조회 중 불이동」 (#104 · `d88dfee`).**
      - Kept as is = ① (⟳ right next to the content at every stage) · ② (stage measurement width = slot width − ⟳ slot − margin) · the purpose (a query does not change the ⟳ slot or the stage).
      - Removed = ③ (stage-3 empty space) · ④ (the measurement copy always counts the marker) — there is no marker to keep. Of the costs in ⑤, 「1·2단이 약 14px 일찍 물러남」 · 「좁은 3단은 조회 중 숫자가 약 14px 일찍 잘림」 · 「쉬는 3단의 약 14px 더 넓은 틈」 disappear. 「값이 바뀌면(자릿수·배지) ⟳ 가 움직인다」 · 「3단 ⟳ 오른쪽 빈 띠는 요약 버튼 밖」 remain.
      - Widened = the stage 1·2 grid column shift that #104 did not address also disappears — the marker widened the name cell (the first column of the grid) so the columns shifted by about 17px during a query (`/qa full` observed 2026-10-01 · screenshot `.claude/handoff/attachments/usage-rev19-qa-2026-10-01/08-popup-codex-refreshing.png` · the stage stayed the same). Now nothing inside the content changes width because of a query.
    - ⑦ **Relation to #106 ②.** The 「그 회사가 갱신 중이면 누름을 버린다」 of ⓑ is changed to ② — there is still no new query and only the rotation spins again (ADR-0261 amends ADR-0259 decision 2 for the on-screen response only). The main proposal that left a place for the 「갱신 중」 text in the header is withdrawn.
    - ⑧ **Tests.**
      - To fix (`UsageSlot.test.tsx` — test names · lines are an aid) — 「「갱신 중」 = ⟳ wait or snapshot in_flight — value as is」 (`:590` → the ⟳ attribute · `aria-busy` · rotation) · 「머리 = … 갱신 중이면 끝에 「갱신 중」 …」 (`:797` → the end of the header is ⟳) · 「↻ 모양(RefreshCw)은 ⟳ 와 갱신 중 표식에만 …」 (`:1085` → only on ⟳) · 「대상의 조회가 도는 동안의 누름은 버린다 …」 (`:1166` → command 0 + rotation restart) · 「3단 — 쉬는 줄은 끝에 갱신 중 표식과 같은 모양·폭의 빈자리 …」 (`:1433`) · 「측정 사본은 조회 여부와 상관없이 같다 …」 (`:1480` → no marker in the copy · stage and ⟳ slot unchanged by a query) · 「갱신 중 표식은 돌고, 숨은 측정 사본은 돌지 않는다」 (`:1677` → the ⟳ icon spins) · the icon-count assertion of the measurement copy (near `:1935`). The summary-name assertion (`:331`) stands as is.
      - New — one full turn even if the query ends quickly · stops at the turn boundary when it ends · re-press during a query → command 0 + rotation from the beginning · press during the tail → command 1 · it also spins for an automatic query (`in_flight` only) · global ⟳ attribute = the list of companies being queried (two · one · none) · per-company ⟳ attribute (that company only) · no attribute or `aria-busy` during the tail. ★jsdom does not run CSS animations★ — the test imitates the turn boundary by firing the event (main inference — the coder confirms).
      - On-screen measurement (`/qa full`) — before and after a query starts and ends, the stage 1·2 grid columns and the x of ⟳ are the same · the rotation turns at least one full turn and stops at the boundary · it spins even under reduced motion.
    - ⑨ **Code places.** `src/components/slot/UsageSlot.tsx` (`RefreshIconButton` · `RefreshButton` · `NameUnit` · `StageRender` stage 3 · `RefreshingMark` · `RefreshingMarkSpace` · `VendorDetail` header · `UsagePopup` comment) · tests `UsageSlot.test.tsx`. Anchor = `// ADR-0261` on the press branch of `RefreshIconButton` (press during a query = no new query + rotation restart · the tail is not during a query) — next to the `// ADR-0258` in that place now.
108. **Amendment 20 (b) — the popup aligns the columns of both companies in one table (user decision 2026-10-01 · option (B) · ADR-0261).**
    - ① **Before the change (code fact — amendment 19 `603248d`).** The window rows (`PopupWindowRow`) were one flex row per row and the name cell was only `minWidth: 3.5em` — a long name (`gpt-reserve`) pushed the bar of that row. The % was neither right-aligned nor fixed-width digits (only the small display grid had `tabular-nums`). The reset time and remaining time were one bundle (`data-usage-reset-at`). The company cells (`<section>` of `VendorDetail`) were separate so the columns of the two companies did not align. The cause of the wide gap above the Fable row was not confirmed (user screenshot). **After the change (`512850d`)** = the one grid of ③④ — the window row is one subgrid row, not flex, and the reset bundle stays but is `display: contents` so it becomes two cells.
    - ② **Columns (user decision).** Name | bar | % (right end) | reset time (`ResetMark` — hourglass + time) | remaining time 「(… 뒤)」 (`data-usage-reset-in`). Name column width = the longest name in the whole popup · fixed-width digits · all row spacing equal · a today-reset row has a blank after the time (accepted).
    - ③ **Main decision (implementation) — one grid.**
      - One CSS grid spanning both company cells holds the five columns — all five column tracks are `auto` (`repeat(5, auto)`). The name column's width is decided by the longest name, and the bar column's by the width of the bar element (`4em` — the bar width before the change as is · no new constant) — `4em` is the element width, not the track width.
      - The company cell (`<section data-usage-popup-vendor>`) inherits the columns of that grid — the coder chose `grid-template-columns: subgrid` (columns only · `grid-column: 1 / -1`) and the user confirmed it on the app screen (2026-10-01 — record = `.claude/handoff/attachments/usage-slot-user-answers-2026-10-01.md` 「구현 확인 (화면)」). The `data-usage-popup-vendor` marker is kept (tests and cdp use it). The table grid is `justify-content: start` — so that even if the popup widens because a status line is long, the `auto` column does not stretch and open a gap between bar and %.
      - The company header row and the 「사용량 페이지 ↗」 row are the full width (`grid-column: 1 / -1`). The top status line and the bottom 「슬롯에 표시」 row stay outside the grid as they are.
      - The window row (`PopupWindowRow`) is also one `subgrid` row that inherits the company cell's columns (columns only · `grid-column: 1 / -1`) — the row element remains and bears the `data-usage-vendor`·`data-usage-window` markers as is. The row indent is given inside the name cell (the row-side margin would mix into the first-column width of the subgrid). Only the reset bundle (`data-usage-reset-at`) is `display: contents` so the time and remaining time become the cells of columns 4 and 5 directly.
      - The number cells (% · time · remaining time) are `tabular-nums` (applied to the whole table grid). Row spacing = the `row-gap` 3px inside a company cell · 8px between company cells (the same as the gap between the other blocks of the popup), and the window row fixes its height at `1.5em` — a row with mixed fonts (a Korean fallback font · Latin names) may have a content height that varies from row to row, so fixing the height keeps the spacing equal regardless of the font. The cause of the gap above the Fable row was never found — the fixed height evens out the spacing regardless of the cause (coder · ①).
    - ④ **Main decision (implementation) — empty cells.** A column number is fixed per cell (the `at()` form of the small display `WindowCells`). So even a row that has no bar because it has no value (「—」) does not have its % pulled into the bar column, and a row with no reset leaves the reset and remaining-time cells empty. The expiry row (「리셋됨 — 갱신 대기」) covers four cells from the bar to the remaining time (the same shape as small-display stages 1·2 — three cells there).
    - ⑤ **DOM text (R27).** The space character between the reset time and the remaining time is kept — so that those who read DOM text (LLM·cdp) do not read the time and remaining time joined together (the visible gap is drawn by the grid · the same reason as #106 ③). The shape in which the reset name (「리셋 HH:MM」 — `title` of `ResetMark`) and the remaining time are in different elements was already decided by #106 ③ (a departure from PRD R28 — the accepted cost as is).
    - ⑥ **Tests.**
      - New — the window rows of both companies are in one grid and use the same column numbers (jsdom does not compute layout — assert by structure and column numbers, and x alignment by on-screen measurement) · the % of a row with no value is in the % column · the expiry row covers four cells · the remaining-time cell of a today-reset row · number cells `tabular-nums`.
      - To fix — tests that assert the popup window-row structure (e.g. 「창별 절대 리셋 시각 …」).
      - On-screen measurement (`/qa full`) — whether the three misalignments in the user's screenshot (the `gpt-reserve` row bar · the `3%` row hourglass · the gap above the Fable row) are gone · the right end of % of both companies and the hourglass are at the same x.
    - ⑦ **Code places and order.** `src/components/slot/UsageSlot.tsx` (`UsagePopup` · `VendorDetail` · `PopupWindowRow`) · tests `UsageSlot.test.tsx`. It is the same file as #107 so one coder does it in order — #107 (⟳ rotation) → #108 (popup table). Wherever it stops between the two, the build and tests stand. Anchor = `// ADR-0261` on the popup grid (do not place per row · name column = the longest name in the whole popup).

109. **Amendment 21 — the refusal deadline is kept in memory only · refusal storage is removed (user decision 2026-10-08 · ADR-0284 · implementation `9ab1523`).** The user: 「구지 저장까지 할 필요는 없다고 생각. 코드만 복잡함」 · survey = `docs/research/usage-reject-persistence-2026-10-08.md`.
    - ① **What was removed.** `usage_service/reject_store.rs` (`RejectStore` · `FileRejectStore` · `MemRejectStore`) · `usage_rejects.json` · reading and restoring at assembly · refusal storage at the end of the completion guard · the dedicated `save_lock`. #30 · the storage wording of #85 · #89 H · §1-4 「거절 저장(R29)」·「조립」·「경로별 락 차례」 · §1-9 #1·#4 · the §4 `RejectStore` row · §6 #6 · §7 #6 are marked with 「ADR-0284 로 걷음」 (struck through, not deleted).
    - ② **Changed behavior.** After a daemon restart it starts with no refusal deadline — if restarted within the deadline, one more query goes out and may receive 429 again (accepted — ADR-0284 「영향 / 불변식」). The in-memory R24 behavior (no automatic or ⟳ query within the deadline · hidden refusal · refusal-end fold) is as is.
    - ③ **The old file.** The `usage_rejects.json` already on disk is neither read nor deleted (ADR-0284 decision 3 — the application of ADR-0264 decision 5).
    - ④ **Locks.** The places that hold locks nested shrink to `명부{책{…}}` one (§1-4 「경로별 락 차례」).
    - ⑤ **Tests.** Daemon lib tests 585 → 566 (20 storage-only tests deleted · 1 「거절이 쿨타임보다 길 때 한 틱에 접고 시작」 test added as the review required) · daemon crate total 927 passed · 0 failed · 4 ignored (measured 2026-10-08 · the tree just before commit 9ab1523).

## 5. Implementation order (build and tests green at every commit — stands wherever it stops)

**Start condition:** none — proceed as is on this branch (user decision 2026-09-27). The parallel branch `v0.3.2/feat/json-midturn-queue` has already been merged into master (`0a67566`) — it added `PROTOCOL_VERSION` 5→6 · agent catalog 4→5 · `StatusSink::inputs_drained` (right after `turn_ended`) and greatly changed codex transport · `output_core` · `connection_core` · `messages.rs` · `protocolClient.ts`. ~~This branch has not yet absorbed it →~~ **when absorbing master or merging this side into master, resolve the conflicts with the checklist below** (numbers are renumbered then as master + 1 — master is already 6, so `PROTOCOL_VERSION` starts from 7). ★Absorption is done — merge `731c6d4` (2026-09-29 · Stage 8 below · step-log)★. When merging this side into master, run the same checklist once more (if master moved in between, renumber).

**Merge checklist (check at each relevant stage):** ① `PROTOCOL_VERSION` = master at merge time + 1 ② agent catalog = master + 1 · shell catalog = master + 1 ③ `MessagingFlushSink`·`UsageObserveSink` pass both `inputs_drained`·`usage_observed` on ④ two new branches in `dispatch_order` (`RefreshUsageLimits` = `Detached` · `UsageSubscribe` = `InOrder`) ⑤ the `command_request_id` branch (`UsageSubscribe` = `None` · `RefreshUsageLimits` = that number) · the `UsageLimitsUpdated` of `event_reply_request_id` = the `None` branch (the ⟳ answer is the existing `Ack` — amendment 6) · the usage arm at the shell `emit_broadcast` call site · the `forward_daemon_command` block branch · the one ⟳ action of the connection task (`sync(.., force=true)` just before the refresh frame — amendment 7) · the two lines `on_connect`·`on_disconnect` of `agent_conn` (the parallel branch greatly changed `messages.rs`·`connection_core`·`protocolClient.ts`) ⑥ regenerate the three generated folders (including the `commands.schema.json` of agent `bindings/` — `agent/tests/ts_export.rs:94`).

1. `S21: feat(agent,protocol): 사용량 중립 타입·wire 도메인 타입 + StatusSink::usage_observed 훅(기본 no-op)` — up to the daemon decorator hand-off · together with ts-rs generated artifacts.
2. `S21: feat(agent): Claude·Codex 줍기 해석 + take_usage · pump drain · UsageGate(비기본 프로필)` — the daemon sink is a no-op, so behavior is unchanged.
3. `S21: feat(agent): 조회 seam + OsProbeSpawner + Claude A·Codex + backend 등록부(정책)` — depends on `chrono`. ★Done = a real Windows smoke with the production command as is succeeds locally + step-log record★.
4-0. `S21: feat(agent,protocol): 한도 정보 없는 계정 = Unavailable 상태 · Claude rate_limits null = 조회 실패` (`84da9c3` — committed) — `Unavailable` state (the sixth wire state) · observation `limits_unavailable` · Claude `rate_limits_available` interpretation (§1-3). ★What remains: `Failed.next_attempt_in_secs` → `u64` is still `Option<u64>` (`domain.rs:500-501`) — carried in Stage 4★.
4-0b. `S21: feat(agent,protocol): 실패 추적 — ProbeFailure·UsageDetail·UpstreamText + wire UsageStateDetail(비정상 상태마다 detail)` (amendment 2) — ★the order in which the build stands★: ① in agent add `UsageDetail`·`UpstreamText` (covering · 200 characters · covered `Debug`)·`ProbeFailure` (+ `From<ProbeError>`), change the `UsageProbe::query` return to `ProbeFailure`, and fit the two queriers · `classify_error` · Codex RPC errors · tests in one commit (the spawner seam is as is, so the `?` place does not change) ② the observation `limits_unavailable: bool → Option<UsageDetail>` and its producer (Claude interpretation) · tests ③ protocol `UsageStateDetail` (hand-implemented `Debug`) + a `detail` cell on the `UsageVendorState` five abnormal ones — there is no consumer yet (the daemon is Stage 4), so only the golden and ts-rs generated artifacts change ④ fix the secret-rule doc of `usage/probe.rs:24-27` as in §1-4 「실패 추적」. This stage does not add a single log line.
4. `S21: feat(protocol,daemon): UsageService — UsageBook(곧장 적용·revision·줍기 합침·detail)·UsageClock·RejectStore(ADR-0284 로 걷음 — 2026-10-08)·요청 판정(+ UsageServed 이전)·합류·완료 가드·구독 명부(watch)·스케줄러 tick + 구동 스레드·출구 포트 UsageOutlet(가짜 시계·조회기·출구 시험)` — ★move `UsageServed` from protocol to `usage_service` and remove the `served` cell of `UsageLimitSnapshot` (amendment 8 — §1-6 · §3 #81)★: the request decision returns `UsageServed` alongside the snapshot · protocol side = cell (`domain.rs:455`) · type (`:513`) · fixture (`:640`) · golden key (`:670`) · ts declaration list (`:732`) · re-export (`lib.rs:40`) · ★`crates/engram-dashboard-protocol/bindings/UsageServed.ts` is deleted by hand in this commit★ — the CI binding sync gate (`git diff --exit-code` after `git add -N -f`) cannot catch a stale file that the generator no longer writes · then regenerate protocol `bindings/` (ts-rs sync gate) — nobody reads the wire before Stage 5, so the build and behavior stand. It is not yet attached to assembly (the scheduler thread is launched by tests and only ending on drop is measured · the decision is a direct call of `tick`). The `u64` switch of `Failed.next_attempt_in_secs` is also here (carried over from 4-0). The waiting map and `inbox.rs` are not built (§3 #36 replaced). `UsageObserveSink` (`observe.rs`) is also built here but is inserted in the chain in Stage 5. The encoder port `UsageEncoder` (`encode(snapshot, subscribed) -> Option<Arc<str>>` · outside the registry lock · only when there are targets — amendment 4 · per distinct set the targets carry — amendment 6) also stands here (amendment 3 — tests use a fake, and the real one comes in Stage 5 together with the wire variant). The registry's target collection takes the exit and a copy of that connection's set together (amendment 6 — the first snapshot of a subscription replacement is the post-replacement set).
5. `S21: feat(protocol,daemon): 사용량 wire — UsageSubscribe(InOrder·request_id 없음)·RefreshUsageLimits(Detached · 답 = 기존 Ack)·UsageLimitsUpdated(+ subscribed) + revision 칸 + PROTOCOL_VERSION + 조립(거절 먼저 로드(ADR-0284 로 걷음 — 2026-10-08)·조회기 주입·스케줄러 기동·UsageObserveSink) + 연결 계층(on_connect attach·on_disconnect detach·ConnUsageOutlet·UsageSubscribe 처리) + 버스 usage.*(blocking · detail 칸)` — `dispatch_order`·`command_request_id` branch · `None` branch must be closed in the same commit as the variant for the build to stand (the ⟳ answer is the existing `Ack`, so there is no new branch on the answer side — amendment 6) (`dispatch_order`·`event_reply_request_id` have no wildcard) · the shell `emit_broadcast` is a `_` arm, so at this stage it silently drops broadcasts, and since the shell does not yet send `UsageSubscribe`, the daemon scheduler does not query (build and behavior both stand — delivery and subscription are Stage 6) · (the removal of the `served` cell and `UsageServed` ended in Stage 4 — amendment 8) fix the doc (`in_flight` · head 「요청형」 `domain.rs:433`) and the `a_usage_observation_is_not_broadcast` doc rationale · fix the `make_table`/`make_daemon_table` call sites (including the eight tests — §1-4) together · the real encoder (wrapping `event_json` with failure = `None` · the registry set as `subscribed` — amendment 6) · the after-deadline result log branch strips every `upstream` key of the payload copy regardless of command name before printing (`command_delivery.rs:1497-1503` + port doc — §1-4 「버스」, amendment 4) · regenerate agent `bindings/` (including `commands.schema.json`) · protocol `bindings/`. ★Done = on a real daemon, `engram usage.get --backend Claude` passes through the blocking entrance and returns a row that is not `OUTCOME_UNKNOWN` (down to the detail cell if it failed)★. **Implementation: `7aad8f7` `4fd426a` (5a) · `56776d6` `c68b885` (5b).**

Only the minimal branch needed for frontend type checking together · the `apply.rs:71-80` `SubscriptionSync` doc notes the usage interest recompute · fix the 「여섯 가지」 sentence at the head of `events.rs` · fix the 「프로세스 밖으로 나가지 않는다」 at the head of `lifecycle.rs:72-76` (amendment 3 — §1-7 「소켓 표식」). **Implementation: `1ee3b29` (6a) `547f83f` (6a fix) · `00e6683` (6b1) `31ca403` (6b2) `0561249` (6b1 fix) · `871bc8f` (6b2 interest recompute wiring) `c1eb031` (6b2 fix) · `6539832` (6c fix — the parent `088266e` is the Stage 7 front commit).**

## 6. Risks, open items and items to confirm

1. **Parallel branch `v0.3.2/feat/json-midturn-queue` — merged into master (`0a67566`)** — two numbers · the `StatusSink` hook · codex transport · `output_core` · `connection_core` · `messages.rs` · `protocolClient.ts` overlap. ~~This branch has not yet absorbed it.~~ This branch absorbed it with merge `731c6d4` (2026-09-29). Response = do not wait for the start (§5), and run the §5 checklist when absorbing or merging master — it ran at absorption time, and once more when merging into master. Claude `mod.rs` is reduced to one skip-arm place + one signature cell, and the probe arguments are not derived from `build_spec` (cost = two sets of arguments — tests measure that the core stream-json arguments match).
2. **Item to confirm — the clock that counts sleep time (R29).** It is not written as fact: whether Rust `Instant` counts sleep time is known to differ by OS (Windows QPC counts it · Linux `CLOCK_MONOTONIC` and macOS do not — unverified). If it differs, that branch is placed only inside one function of the `UsageClock` real implementation. ★The scheduler's wait (std `recv_timeout`) carries the same question★ — if the wait clock does not count sleep, it wakes late after resume → the `SCHEDULE_MAX_SLEEP = 60초` cap bounds that lateness to 60 seconds, and the decision after waking uses `UsageClock::mono` (§1-4).
3. **Production launch path** — EXPERIMENTAL · the "not supported in this context" branch (mode not identified) · allowlist env · `bypassPermissions` removal · `--settings <파일>` differs from the probe · `cmd.exe /c claude` shim · 9009 decision · POSIX tree kill and stdin EOF behavior unverified → the local smoke of the Stage 3 completion condition confirms them.
4. **The error surface of A — partly resolved (2026-09-27)** — logout is not an error but a success response `rate_limits_available: false` · `rate_limits: null` (measured — `agent/tests/usage_probe_smoke.rs:72`) → `Unavailable`. 429 comes inside `rate_limits: null` according to the CLI 2.1.280 schema description and code reading (no error subtype — not measured) → `Upstream` (§6 #21). The wording table of §3 #6 is still an estimate — it is used only when another version answers with an error subtype.
5. **Codex** — the `account/rateLimits/read` params shape (`request_line` always carries `params` `protocol.rs:278-288`) · the not-logged-in error shape are confirmed by schema re-extraction (`protocol.rs:3-6`) · `initialize` about 9.7 seconds observed once in resuming a conversation right after a daemon restart (cause unknown — the 30-second deadline covers it) · notification arrival frequency unknown (UI §9) · `accountId` logging forbidden.
6. ~~**Tension between refusal storage and the clock (accepted)** — only a wait that crosses a restart is wall clock, so moving the clock between restarts skews the restored wait (`REJECT_MAX` is the cap).~~ ★Resolved — refusal storage was removed by ADR-0284 (2026-10-08)★: there is no wait that crosses a restart, so there is no such tension either. Instead, if the daemon is restarted within the refusal deadline, one more query may go out (ADR-0284 「영향 / 불변식」 cost).
7. **Gap in the expiry latch (narrowed by amendment 2)** — for a subscribed company, the scheduler wakes at `resets_at` to set the latch and broadcast (at the latest `SCHEDULE_MAX_SLEEP`). The remaining gap = a company whose reset has passed **while nobody subscribes** — it stands at the next subscription add · request · pickup (subscription add evaluates before the first snapshot). If the clock is rewound before that, the old % may be visible — the PRD §4 R32 item was narrowed to 「리셋 경과를 데몬이 확인한 뒤」.
8. **Account (D13)** — the query sees only the default login of the daemon env (`config_dir()` `mod.rs:993-1000`). Non-default profile agents were also blocked from pickup (§1-2). ★If the account is changed with `/login`, an agent that is already running may pick up old-account values until restart★ — they mix with queried values for a while.
9. **Mismatch between the instruction and the code — wait cap.** 「기한이 지나면 조회하고 답한다」 collides with the bus deadline of 7 seconds and the shell reply cap of 30 seconds (Claude 15-second and Codex 30-second deadlines). Minimum adjustment = cache after `REPLY_WAIT_MAX` + `in_flight` (§3 #16) · the wire ⟳ request `Detached` (§3 #15). After amendment 2, this cap applies only to ⟳ and the bus — the subscription path does not wait.
10. **Places that diverge from the skeleton (review confirmation requested)** — ① StatusSink hook + pump drain ② the bus declaration is in the agent block (synchronous port) ③ three number bumps ④ two cells in `ProbeError` ⑤ `checked` in the ADR-0064 schema (§7 #8) ⑥ opener permission = an app permission setting change ⑦ LLM `setSlotContent` partial patch ⑧ `(vendor, account_key)` key ⑨ the webview uses a dedicated wire request ⑩ `REPLY_WAIT_MAX` ⑪ the new decorator `UsageObserveSink` (old name `UsageInboxSink`) ⑫ change broadcast `UsageLimitsUpdated` + `revision` (user decision 2026-09-27) ⑬ the sixth state `Unavailable` ⑭ subscription type — wire `UsageSubscribe` · the daemon subscription registry and scheduler thread · the shell's interest authority · cache · resend on a new socket (user decision 2026-09-27 — §3 #54–#67) → amendment 6: the shell computes interest from the layout · reconciliation of the broadcast's `subscribed` · forced resend before ⟳ (user decision 2026-09-27 — §3 #78) ⑮ failure detail and upstream raw text + amendment of the `ProbeError` secret rule (user decision 2026-09-27 — §3 #68–#72).
11. **`PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump** — if an old daemon is running, it takes the existing mismatch handling (discovery `check_acceptable`).
12. **Multi-daemon** — queries per daemon share the same account's query limit · the Claude response has no account identifier · out of scope. The single-instance guard is **one per data folder** (`net/src/instance.rs:1-5`·`:216`), so even on the same PC two can run if the folders differ (release data folder = `data/` next to the exe, overridable with env — `discovery/src/lib.rs:80-87`). ~~The refusal file is also separate per folder.~~ (the refusal file was removed by ADR-0284 — 2026-10-08)
13. **Strong query limit** — even 15 minutes is not an interval proven safe (query §0-3). Whether A and B use the same bucket is unconfirmed (§0-4). ~~⟳ is blocked only by the 30-second interval.~~ ⟳ is blocked only by joining an in-progress query and the block during refusal (§3 #102 ③ · §6 #21 — on Claude even the block during refusal does not reach).
14. **Three copies of the console-window flag** — `CREATE_NO_WINDOW` lives inline in three places (the existing two + the spawner module). Unification is a separate matter.
15. **Multi-client out of scope (user decision)** — subscription is per connection so even if several clients attach each receives its own and the query runs as one daemon union, but it is not a target of design or verification. PRD R29 was corrected to 「모든 사용량 슬롯이 공유」 (2026-09-27).
16. ~~**Pickup → screen delay** — since there is no broadcast, a value picked up during a conversation appears at the next request (≤ 60 seconds) (accepted)~~ — ★replaced (user decision 2026-09-27)★: a changed value is broadcast at once (§3 #47). ~~The remaining delay = until the latch set at request time and the query start `in_flight` reach another webview (≤ 60-second poll)~~ — in amendment 2 that too is broadcast. The remaining delay = pickup merge ≤ 1 second.
17. **Pickup↔query order (no rule — user decision 2026-09-27)** — because of the CLI's 1-minute shared cache, a query result may briefly overwrite a value just picked up — the harm = it briefly looks like slightly more remains · if it is strange in real use, add a rule. The reset time varies below the decimal point from call to call (measured), so a 60-second tolerance is already used in the 「같은 리셋」 comparison (§3 #46) — if an order rule is added, it uses the same constant. ★Amendment 12 (#88) leaves this as is★ — observation times are not compared, and under the book lock the later write wins in arrival order (a difference under 1 second is not told apart by design). Since the source is not distinguished, there is no priority between a query answer and a picked-up value either.
18. **Query cwd in a development build** — the temp folder is under the data folder, so in a development build it may sit inside the repository → claude may climb up and read `.claude/settings.json` · CLAUDE.md · `.mcp.json` (hooks and MCP are already blocked). Run the Stage 3 real smoke once with the data folder inside the repository to confirm.
19. **The response of Codex API-key login is unmeasured (open)** — with today's code it is likely (estimated) to become `Parse` → a permanent 「조회 실패」. Add the API-key login case to the Codex smoke, gather the shape, and then decide whether to fold it into `Unavailable`.
20. **`Unavailable` wording (Stage 7)** — the cause set includes **a login token without profile permission** (inferred from the schema description — not measured). A wording that speaks only of 「로그아웃했거나 한도가 없는 계정」 would mislead such a user → set the wording so that it does not assert the cause.
21. **`Rejected` is unreachable on Claude 2.1.280** — the CLI-side 429 comes as `rate_limits: null` and shows as `Failed` (§1-3). The automatic retry is at the normal cooldown (15 minutes ≥ `REJECT_FALLBACK` 5 minutes), so the automatic path is not more aggressive, but ★`Failed` does not block ⟳★ — even during a limit, ⟳ may go out ~~at 30-second intervals (`REFRESH_MIN_SPACING`)~~ every time it is pressed (an in-progress one is joined — the interval was deleted in amendment 15) (interpretation). If it is a problem in real use, a separate decision. ★Amendment 15 accepted this risk (§3 #102)★.
22. **Order assumption of revision reset — closed with the socket marker in amendment 3 · the connection-state forgetting removed in amendment 4** — the old shape relied on the order (`connection.rs:728`·`:841`) that the frontend forgets the revision when it leaves `connected` and that the shell reads that socket's broadcasts only after it emits the new socket's `Connected`, and whether Tauri events arrive in emit order across names was unverified. Amendment 2 backed the discarded first snapshot with 「새 데몬은 기준점이 없어 곧바로 조회해 몇 초 안에 메운다」 but ★there were two holes (codex finding)★: ① if there is a stored refusal, the new daemon does not query (§1-9 #1) so there is no way to fill it ② generation succession (Connected→Connected — `daemon_client/mod.rs:395-398`·`connection.rs:702-709`) does not take the connection state out of `connected`, so forgetting itself does not happen. ★Amendment 3 = the shell socket marker `socket_epoch` on the usage payload going to the webview (§1-7 「소켓 표식」 · §3 #75)★ — forgetting and the first payload of the new socket happen inside one `merge`, so the event-order assumption is gone. ★Amendment 4 — connection-state forgetting was removed (codex re-review)★: amendment 3 kept it as the 「잊기만 하니 해가 없는」 second rule, but if a late-arriving disconnect transition erases the new socket's revision floor, a late-arriving small revision of the same socket is accepted (§1-8 store) — forgetting alone was harmful. The remaining assumption = the socket marker is never rewound inside one shell process (`lifecycle.rs:78-81` — the code keeps it).
23. **Widening the shell notification port** — the head of `DaemonEvents` is 「여섯 가지가 전부 — 넓히지 말 것」 (`src-tauri/src/daemon_client/events.rs:68-70`). The seventh (`usage_limits_updated(snapshot, labels, socket_epoch)` — §1-7 「방송 전달」 · §3 #51) is a 1:1 delivery of one daemon broadcast, and for `labels`·`socket_epoch` the call site only passes the result of the pure module and its own socket marker, so it was judged not to be the target of that warning (a substitute for AppHandle). ★1:1 also stands by the position of the arm (amendment 4)★ — `emit_broadcast` (`connection.rs:1228`) is a synchronous function that receives only `(events, ev)` and has no interest state, socket marker or `sink`, so the usage arm stands at its call site (`:1015` — the connection task that holds `sink`·`socket_epoch`). Reconciliation (`on_snapshot`) and resend (`resend` → `sink`) are the share of the call site outside the port, and the port method receives only computed values, once per broadcast — a Stage 6 review confirmation item.
24. **Saturation treatment of the per-connection `try_send` (amendment 2 · made explicit in amendment 3)** — emit was moved from all-connection fan-out (saturation = skip) to a per-connection exit (saturation = that connection ends) (§3 #59). ★The treatment is the same as the agent output subscription sink (`FrameSink::try_send` saturation = an out-of-queue end signal — `net/src/frame_port.rs:43-50`), and differs from list broadcasts (`AgentListUpdated`·`PresetListUpdated` etc. — all-connection fan-out skips only that connection on saturation and `warn`, `net/src/ws.rs:210-221`)★. A dropped connection reconnects → the shell sends interest back and receives the first snapshot. Cost = one reconnection + a full replay of the agent output that connection was watching — an existing cost already paid at every disconnect (ADR-0046 decision 2 — only the trigger for the claim was moved to the registry observation by ADR-0164), so it is accepted. A snapshot is small and rare, so the new risk is judged to be small.
25. **A connection with no subscription at all is not reconciled (accepted — narrowed in amendment 6)** — shell → daemon divergence is now caught by the broadcast's `subscribed` reconciliation (§1-7 「대조」 · user decision 2026-09-27): for both the short side (one growth sheet lost — shell command channel saturated `daemon_client/mod.rs:879-881` or refused by the daemon's saturated receiving line) and the excess side (one shrink sheet lost), if a snapshot arrives on that connection **even for another company**, it is fixed at that one sheet — the cost of the excess side ≤ one query. There is no webview → shell bridge (the shell computes interest from the layout — the old report loss and ordering problems vanished altogether, §3 #78). ★The remaining gap = when a connection has **no** subscription at all★ (e.g. the one sheet of the first growth of an empty interest vanishes) — no snapshot arrives so there is no occasion to reconcile, and the slot looks empty. ★The way to fix it★: ⟳ fixes it — the remedy for the lost growth sheet is ⟳ (the connection task sends the interest back on the same socket right before the refresh frame · the two are one action so one of them cannot vanish alone on the shell side — §1-7 「⟳」, amendment 7). ★The gap that remains even after ⟳ (amendment 8)★: if the daemon refuses the `UsageSubscribe` written right before ⟳ (receiving line saturated), the refresh `Ack` arrives but that company's value does not come — if an event of **another** subscribed company arrives on that connection, its `subscribed` reveals the divergence and the resend heals it, and if no other company emits events, it remains until ⟳ again · a layout change that changes interest · a new socket. A layout change fixes it **only when it was a shell-side nudge loss** — because `sent` did not change, the next recompute issues the nudge again (a nudge carries no value so one sheet fully fills the earlier loss — §1-7 「한 직렬 경로」). When the daemon refused a sheet written to the socket, `sent` has already changed, so only a change that changes interest or ⟳ fixes it. A new socket always fixes it. A periodic full resend (30 seconds) was rejected by the user (§2). A nudge loss in the gap before `cmd_tx` is saved is filled by the nudge right after saving (amendment 4 — §1-7 「새 소켓」).
26. **Destination of upstream raw text (marker only)** — the raw text does not go to logs (the after-deadline result log also strips the `upstream` key regardless of command name — §1-4 「버스」 · amendment 4), but goes to the UI and the LLM read path (the `usage.get` row) — the LLM row enters the model context. Covering is only the token shapes the base masker knows (`base/src/logging/mod.rs:49`) · the raw-text cell holds only named cell values (§3 #72). What goes into the raw text is decided by the CLI version — no policy judgment is made (the same marker as PRD D3).
27. **The `i64` cell of the bus macro was unconfirmed** — it was not confirmed whether the declaration macro (`command/src/macros.rs`) accepts `UsageVendorRow.detail_code: Option<i64>`. If it does not, spread it out as a string cell (Stage 5). ★Closed (Stage 5 implementation) — it accepts★: the macro accepted the `i64` cell as is and it was carried as `detail_code: Option<i64>` (`commands.rs:313`·`:347`). ★But as a result the TS-side types diverge (§3 #96 — awaiting user decision)★ — the generated `bindings/UsageVendorRow.ts` is `detail_code?: bigint | null`, whereas the wire type `UsageStateDetail.code` that carries the same value was fixed by hand as `number | null` (`domain.rs` — protocol does not use the ts-rs default `i64 → bigint` mapping). The bus row and the wire snapshot carry the same number with different JS types.
28. ~~**A minimized window counts as visible (amendment 3 — a main confirmation item)**~~ — ★closed (user decision 2026-09-27 — §2)★: a minimized window counts as a visible window — the query continues and only tray hiding (`hide_main_ui`) removes interest (§1-7 「숨김」 · §3 #76). It is a decided behavior, not a gap (「최소화할때 뭐 안한다는 기준서면 모든걸 다 그렇게 처리해야되잖아」). Cost = even while minimized, one query per company every 15 minutes. No path for the shell to observe minimize transitions is built.
29. ~~**Whose age is the 「N분 전」 on the popup header (amendment 19 — ★awaiting user confirmation★)** — the age comes per window, not per company (`UsageWindow.age_secs` = 「이 값을 관측한 뒤 흐른 초」). The merge overwrites only the windows carried (#88), so if pickup carries only one window, the ages of the two windows diverge. Candidates = the newest window / the oldest window (cost = §3 #106 ② ⓐ). Amber (④) also uses this answer as input. Until the answer, the header age of ② is not built (§5 9).~~ ★Closed (user decision — main recommendation B · user answer 2026-09-30 (this session — original = `.claude/handoff/attachments/usage-slot-user-answers-2026-09-30.md`))★: it is **the oldest age** of the two windows, 5-hour and weekly, and per-model windows are not included · amber is the same value. ★The old description 「갈리는 경우는 창 하나만 실은 줍기 뒤뿐」 was wrong (review finding)★ — that holds between the 5-hour and weekly windows, and per-model windows are filled only by the query so they may age by hours while a conversation continues (code place = §3 #106 ② ⓐ · ADR-0259 decision 1).
30. **Amber on e-ink (amendment 19 — main observation · handling undecided)** — the amber base token `--status-blocked` is `var(--text)` on e-ink (`src/styles/theme.css:3`). So an old age only becomes darker than the usual dim text (`--text-muted`) with no color cue. Whether to add a shape cue as on the bar (R11 precedent — hatching · thick border) was not decided.
31. **The hourglass may be read as remaining time (amendment 19 — accepted risk · watch in real use)** — ADR-0258 rejected the 「시각만」 notation as 「00:58 은 남은 시간으로 읽힌다」. The hourglass was used for remaining-time display among peers (survey `docs/research/usage-freshness-reset-label-toggle-peers-2026-09-30.md` ③ — Usage4Claude · likely). There is no precedent of using it for a reset time, and the user was told that fact. The word 「리셋」 remains only in `title` and the accessible name (§3 #106 ③). ★It shrank but did not disappear (main reasoning · ADR-0259 「거부한 대안」)★ — the hourglass is read as 「이 시각까지」, which fits the popup showing the remaining time 「(N 뒤)」 together.
32. **The small display alone does not tell which company is being queried (amendment 20 — accepted cost · main observation)** — the single global ⟳ spins if even one of the targets is being queried (§3 #107 ②). When only one company is slow (e.g. the Codex 30-second deadline), the small display does not show which one. What covers it = the popup's per-company ⟳ (only that company spins) · the per-company 「갱신 중」 in the summary button's accessible name · the `data-usage-refreshing` company list of the global ⟳ (§3 #107 ④). A failure leaves the 「!」 badge (PRD R31). At decision time the main session raised this cost as an opinion (the popup's per-company ⟳ shows per-company progress) and the user chose (A) (§2).
33. **A stopped ⟳ looks the same as a resting ⟳ (amendment 20 — accepted risk · main observation)** — the removed marker existed only during a query, so even when the rotation stopped, 「있다」 was itself the signal. ⟳ has the same shape at rest and while spinning, so if the rotation does not spin for some reason (browser policy · render delay), the on-screen progress signal vanishes altogether. The agent rotation symbol (`src/components/agent/agentGlyph.css` comment) left headroom so that it reads from its shape alone, but ⟳ has no such headroom. What remains = `aria-busy` · `data-usage-refreshing` · the cursor (`progress` — `RefreshIconButton`). Making it spin even under the reduced-motion setting (§3 #107 ②) blocks the path by which a setting would stop it. Watch in real use.

## 7. ADR proposals (numbers are given by `/adr`)

1. **Usage = a `(회사, 계정)`-unit state owned by the daemon, vendor policy in the backend registry** — rejected: a per-agent `OutputEvent` variant (per-agent and a replay target, so the lifetimes differ) · shell ownership (several windows would count one account separately) · vendor constants and enums on the daemon side (a violation of 「백엔드 확장」).
2. ~~**The usage query timing is the request (pull) type — daemon cache + refresh when stale**~~ — ★replaced by #10 (user decision 2026-09-27 — a reversal on the same day)★. Kept as a record: the cost of 「수요 신호 + 방송으로 조회 시점을 정하기」 rejected then (reconnection resend · popout cleanup · daemon timer) is paid by #10 — the active-tab derivation is done by the shell from the layout (amendment 6 — it was once the webview's visibility decision · §3 #78). The rejection of 「클라이언트가 쿨타임을 세어 조회 시점을 정하는 타이머」 stays in #10 as well (the daemon counts the cooldown).
3. **Pickup exit = StatusSink hook + pump drain + apply straight to the book from the pump thread (leaf lock · emit outside the lock)** — 2026-09-27 amendment (attached to #9). Rejected: `open_spawn` port injection · `Structured` outlet (`decoder.rs:18-25`) · daemon waiting map + merge at request time (the old decision — replaced by the user decision 2026-09-27: pickup cannot reach the screen until the next request) · a global FIFO queue (overflow dropping · reversal of drain order). The old rejected item 「pump 스레드에서 책 락 잡고 병합」 became the adopted side with this amendment — because that lock is a leaf with no I/O, await or other lock.
4. **Claude active query = A (`get_usage` on a short-lived CLI)** — user decision 2026-09-27. Rejected for now: B (token file + private OAuth usage address) — the app would touch credentials, and a first TLS dependency, internet sending and per-OS credential branches would newly appear. If A breaks, B is brought in behind the same seam.
5. **Active query = a short-lived dedicated process, detached thread + Job-close kill** — rejected: sending to a running agent process (D7) · a generic RPC surface on the conversation channel (`transport/mod.rs:51-90`) · an explicit shutdown step (cannot cover a crash).
6. **Clock = elapsed time including sleep time, ~~only refusal on the wall clock to disk (24-hour cap)~~** — rejected: all wall clock (the wait grows and shrinks on a rewind) · ~~no storage (저장 안 함) (a restart hits the address that is refusing again)~~. ★「저장 안 함」 was adopted by ADR-0284 (2026-10-08 · user decision) — its cost (a restart may hit the address that is refusing again) was accepted★
7. **`usage.*` = an agent block declaration (next to the producer — ADR-0155 decision 1) + synchronous port + `blocking_handler`** — rejected: the daemon's own block + table composition (composition newly laid on three surfaces) · an async handler (the daemon entrance makes it into `OUTCOME_UNKNOWN`).
8. **`SlotMenuItem.checked` — an additive amendment of the ADR-0064 fixed schema** (ADR-0065 precedent) — scope = adding the `checked` cell only, with state entering through the menu ctx. Rejected: putting a state character in the title (the title is fixed at build time) · re-registering the menu on every toggle · a contribution reading the view store directly (ADR-0064 「메뉴에서 직접 store 호출 금지」).
9. **Usage value changes are pushed out at once (`UsageLimitsUpdated` + `revision` per cell)** — user decision 2026-09-27 (「이왕 빨리 전달할 수 있으면 즉각즉각이 맞지싶어」). ★Target and trigger changed in amendment 2★: target = connections that subscribed to that company (per-connection exit — not an all-connection fan-out, §3 #59) · trigger = whenever the revision rises (only pickup merged to 1 second per company + final emit guaranteed). The frontend discards a smaller revision and forgets the revision only when the shell socket marker (`socketEpoch`) grows (amendments 3·4 — it does not forget on a disconnect transition). Peer basis = t3code · Orca · Codex TUI · CodexMeter — merge a push source on arrival (`docs/research/usage-limit-display-ui-survey-2026-09-26.md:89-108`). Rejected: apply at the next request (the old waiting map — pickup → screen ≤ 60 seconds · the cost of the old #2).
10. **Usage = subscription type — a declarative company set per connection, the daemon scheduler counts the cooldown and reset to query and emit** (replaces #2) — user decision 2026-09-27 (「클라는 구독요청 해제 이벤트를 보내고 … 서버쪽에 관리가 들어간이상 15분 쿨타임도 서버쪽에서 관리하는게 맞아보임」 · 「클라가 창 없으면 구독 해제 이벤트 보내는걸로」). Composition = wire `UsageSubscribe{vendors}` (whole replacement) · daemon subscription registry + scheduler thread (queries only subscribed companies) · subscription add = first snapshot to that connection · ★amendment 6 (user decision 2026-09-27): interest = the shell computes it from its own layout (the companies turned on by usage slots in the active tab of visible windows — 「셸이 데이터만 들고있고 웹뷰는 그것기반으로 그려주는거잖아」) · every broadcast carries that connection's `subscribed` and the shell reconciles (「클로드만 와도 상태플래그 하나 더 와서 안맞으면 싱크」) · ⟳ = forced subscription resend, then a query request (「체크온오프하고 동기화버튼 정도로 매칭」)★ · shell = shrink delay (ends at the deadline) · cache hand-off (the webview pulls) · resend on a new socket · one serial transmission path and `sent` · socket marker `socket_epoch` · tray hide is removed by the shell · the nudge right after `cmd_tx` is saved · minimized = visible (user decision) · ⟳ answer = the existing `Ack` (`pending` only). Rejected (user): frontend 60-second poll + decision at request time (if the server holds the cooldown and only the trigger is held by the client, polling and delay remain) · full resend every 30 seconds (amendment 6 — 「사이드 이슈때문에 30초마다 매번 보내는걸 추가한다고?」). Rejected (internal choice — §3): ★the webview computes interest, reports it to the shell, and keeps the order as a rule (seq · load_gen · StaleGen reissue · shrink healing — amendments 3–5 · #78)★ — it was a second copy of the layout the shell already has (the lesson of ADR-0046) and at every re-review a new ordering hole came out of that path · paired subscribe/unsubscribe messages (#54 — a lost unsubscribe = the query continues) · sending the first snapshot while holding the lock (#57) · a tokio task scheduler (#58) · all-connection broadcast (#59) · the shell emitting to all windows without filtering (#63) · each caller reading the set and sending it (#74) · forgetting the revision on a connection-state transition alone (#75) · a connection-state transition together as a second forgetting rule (#75 — amendment 4) · hide reporting by the webview `visibilitychange` (#76) · building JSON per connection (#79) · computing interest at the layout notification (#83) · a daemon-read hand-off instead of the shell cache (when a new window asks for values, the shell sends a read-only wire request and delivers its answer only to that window — #62 · amendment 5 · main decision · condition for reconsideration = move if the cache rules grow beyond the current four).
11. **Failure tracing = classification word + upstream raw text, raw text only to the UI and LLM** — user decision 2026-09-27 (「오류 문구 원문이 있으면 보내야지 … 이상한거 다 섞어서 보내지 말고 타입은 보내야지」 · 「유저가 최대한 추적할 수 있도록」). Composition = wire `UsageStateDetail{kind, code, upstream}` on every abnormal state · agent `ProbeFailure`·`UsageDetail`·`UpstreamText` (covering · 200 characters · covered `Debug`) · amendment of the `ProbeError` secret rule. Rejected (user): mixing several sources into one string without classification. Rejected (main): raw text in logs too (logs may be shared). Rejected (internal — §3): `kind` enum (#68 — an old shell's deserialization breaks) · a cell per variant (#70).
12. **Usage values do not distinguish the source (pickup · query)** — user decision 2026-09-28 (「유저에게는 조회 줍기 구분하지말고 그냥 주고 데몬도 거기에 맞춰서 구성해」). Composition = one merge rule (what is carried overwrites, what is not carried is kept) · an observation holding a window value goes to `Ready` from any state (the refusal deadline remains inside) · no source in the wire snapshot · D12 source-independent (§3 #88). What only the query gives = account state (installed · login · no limit information · 429). Rejected (user): distinguishing the source so that only the query sets the state — the user does not need to know the source, and a new value coming means it is being updated. It replaces PRD D18 (query null or missing = "없음").
